''kollip puṉatta akilcumantu kalpāyntu
vāṉiṉ aruvi tatumpak kaviṉiya
nāṭaṉ nayamuṭiyaṉ eṉpataṉāl nīppiṉum
vāṭal maṟantaṉa tōḷ.''	(aintiṇiyeḻu. 2)
nayamuṭiya ṉeṉṟataṉāṉ varivu talivantami yaṟintu kūṟiṉāḷām.
''ilipaṭar taṇkuḷavi yēynta potumpiṟ
kuliyuṭik kāntaḷ iṉavaṇ ṭimirum
variyaka nāṭaṉum vantāṉmaṟ ṟaṉṉi
aliyum alipōyiṟ ṟiṉṟu.''	(aintiṇiyeḻu. 3)
kaḷavaṟivuṟṟavaḻik kūṟiya ceyyuḷ
''yāṅkā kuvamō aṇinutaṟ kuṟumakaḷ
tēmpaṭu cāral ciṟutiṉip peruṅkural
cevvāyp piṅkiḷi kavara nīmaṟṟu
evvāyc ceṉṟaṉi avaṇeṉak kūṟi
aṉṉi yāṉāḷ kaḻaṟamuṉ ṉiṉṟu
aruvi ārkkum peruvari nāṭaṉi
aṟiyalum aṟiyēṉ kāṇṭalum ilaṉē
vetirpuṉi taṭṭiyēṉ malarpūk koytu
cuṉipāyn tāṭiṟṟum ilaṉeṉa niṉivili
poyyal antō vāyttaṉi yatukēṭṭut
taliyiṟiñ ciṉaḷē yaṉṉi
celavoḻin taṉiyā laḷiyi nīpuṉattē.''	(naṟ. 147)
eṉa varum.
tamartaṟkātta kāraṇap pakkattiṟkuk kūṟiya ceyyuḷ
''perunīr aḻuvat tenti tanta'' eṉṉuṅ kaḷiṟṟiyāṉi niriyuḷ,
''palpūṅ kāṉal alkiṉam varutal
kavvi nallaṇaṅ kuṟṟa ivvūrk
koṭitaṟi peṇṭir coṟkoṇ ṭaṉṉi
kaṭikoṇ ṭaṉaḷē tōḻi peruntuṟi
elliyum iravum eṉṉātu kalleṉa
valavaṉ āynta vaṇpari
nilavumaṇal koṭkumōr tēruṇ ṭeṉavē.''	(akam. 20)
eṉa varum.
taṉkuṟi taḷḷiya teruḷāk kāli vantavaṉ peyarnta vaṟuṅkaḷam nōkkit taṉpiḻippākat taḻīit tēṟiyataṟkuc ceyyuḷ
'iruḷkiḻippatu pōl' eṉṉum kaḷiṟṟiyāṉi niriyuḷ,
''vāḷnaṭan taṉṉa vaḻakkaruṅ kavali
uḷḷunar uṭkuṅ kallaṭarc ciṟuneṟi
aruḷpuri neñcamō ṭeḵkutuṇi yāka
vantōṉ koṭiyaṉum allaṉ tanta
nītava ṟuṭiyiyum alli niṉvayiṉ 
āṉā arumpaṭar ceyta
yāṉē tōḻi tavaṟuṭi yēṉē.''	(akam. 72)
eṉa varum.
vaḻuviṉṟu... aṉṉavumuḷavē eṉpatu vaḻuviṉṟu niliiya iyaṟpaṭuporuṇ mutalāka 'ēmañcāṉṟa uvaki' yīṟākac collappaṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷil taṉṉiṭattu urimiyum avaṉiṭattup parattimiyum aṉṉaviyum nikaḻappeṟum e-ṟu.
aṉṉa eṉpatu  avipōlvaṉa e-ṟu.
ōriṭattukkaṇ eṉṟataṉāṉ ivvāṟu ellārmāṭṭum evviṭattum nikaḻātu eṉṟavāṟām. eṉavē mēṟ 'kuṟippiṉum iṭattiṉu mallatu' (kaḷaviyal. 18) kūṟṟu nikaḻāteṉpataṉi maṟuttu ōriṭattuk kūṟṟu nikaḻum eṉṟavāṟām.
avaṟṟuḷ, vaḻuviṉṟu niliiya iyaṟpaṭu poruḷiṉum eṉpatu talivaṉi iyaṟpaḻittavaḻi avaṉ kuṟṟamilaṉāka niliniṟuttappaṭṭa iyaṟpaṭamoḻinta poruṇmikkaṇṇum taṉvayiṉ urimi tōṉṟavum avaṉvayiṟ parattimi tōṉṟavum kūṟum talivi e-ṟu.
iraṇṭiṉuḷ oṉṟu tōṉṟa urikku meṉṟavāṟu. eṉavē iraṇṭun tōṉṟa varuvaṉavu muḷavām.
poḻutu māṟum... cintikkaṇṇum eṉpatu  talivaṉ varuṅ kālamum iṭaṉum kuṟṟamuḷavātalāṉ, āṇṭu aḻivuvanta cintikkaṇṇum talimakaḷ taṉvayiṉ urimiyum avaṉvayiṟ parattimiyum tōṉṟak kūṟum e-ṟu.
kāmañ ciṟappiṉum eṉpatu  talimakaṉmāṭṭu vēṭki mikiṉum taṉvayiṉ urimiyum avaṉvayiṟ parattimiyum urikkum talivi e-ṟu.
avaṉ aḷi ciṟappiṉum eṉpatu  talivaṉ taliyaḷi mikka vaḻiyum taṉvayiṉ urimiyum avaṉvayiṟ parattimiyum tōṉṟak kūṟum e-ṟu.
ēmañcāṉṟa uvakikkaṇṇum eṉpatu  ēmam poruntiya makiḻcci vantuḻiyum taṉvayiṉ urimiyum avaṉvayiṟ parattimi yum tōṉṟak kūṟum e-ṟu. aḵtāvatu ivaṉ varintallatu nīṅkāṉ eṉṉum uvaki.
avaṟṟuḷ, vaḻuviṉṟu niliiya iyaṟpaṭu
poruṭkaṇ kūṟiyataṟkuc ceyyuḷ
''aṭumpama laṅkoṭi uḷputin toḻippa
veṇmalar virikkun taṇṇan tuṟivaṉ
koṭiyaṉ āyiṉum āka
avaṉē tōḻieṉ ṉuyirkā valaṉē.''	(aiṅkuṟu. taṉi. 6)
eṉa varum.
poḻutu māṟum puriva taṉmiyiṉ aḻivutali vanta cintiyāṉ talivi kūṟiya ceyyuḷ
''koṭuvari vēṅki piḻittuk kōṭpaṭṭu
maṭicevi vēḻam iriya  aṭiyōci
añci yotuṅkum ataruḷḷi āriruḷ
tuñcā cuṭarttoṭi kaṇ.''	(aintiṇiyim. 16)
''vaḷivāyc ciṟukiḷi viḻitiṉi kaṭiyac
celkeṉ ṟāḷē aṉṉi eṉanī
colliṉ evaṉō tōḻi kolli
neṭuṅki vaṉmāṉ kaṭumpakal uḻanta
kuṟuṅki irumpulik kōḷval ēṟṟi
piṅkaṭ cennāy paṭupatam pārkkum
āriruḷ naṭunāḷ varuti
cāral nāṭa vāralō eṉavē.''	(kuṟun. 141)
eṉa varum.
kāma mikkavaḻik kūṟiya ceyyuḷ
''amma vāḻi tōḻi nalaṉē
iṉṉa tākutal koṭitē puṉṉi
aṇimalar tuṟitoṟu virikkum
maṇinīrc cērppaṉi maṟavā tōrkkē.''	(aiṅkuṟu. 117)
taṉvayiṉ urimi:
''naḷḷeṉ ṟaṉṟē yāmañ collavintu
iṉitaṭaṅ kiṉarē mākkaḷ muṉiviṉṟi
naṉantali ulakamun tuñcum
ōryāṉ maṉṟa tuñcā tēṉē.''	(kuṟun. 6)
avaṉvayiṟ parattimi:
''koṭuntāḷ alava kuṟiyām irappēm
oṭuṅkā olikaṭaṟ cērppaṉ  neṭuntēr
kaṭanta vaḻiyieṅ kaṇṇārak kāṇa
naṭantu citiyāti nī.''	(aintiṇiyim. 42)
avaṉaḷi ciṟantavaḻit talivi kūṟiya ceyyuḷ
''cēṇōṉ māṭṭiya naṟumpuki ñekiḻi
vāṉa mīṉiṉ vayiṉvayiṉ imikkum
ōṅkumali nāṭaṉ cāntupular akalam
uḷḷiṉ uḷnōy malkum
pulliṉ māyva tevaṉkol aṉṉāy.''	(kuṟun. 150)
''maṉṟap palaviṉ cuḷiviḷi tīmpaḻam
uṇṭuvantu manti mulivaruṭak  kaṉṟamarn (tu)
āmā curakkum aṇimali nāṭaṉi
yāmāp piriva tilam.''	(aintiṇiyeḻu. 4)
eṉa varum.
ēmañ cāṉṟa uvakikkaṇ kūṟiya ceyyuḷ
''ōṅkal iṟuvarimēṟ kāntaḷ kaṭikaviṉap
pāmpeṉa ōṭi urumiṭippak kaṇṭiraṅkum
pūṅkuṉṟa nāṭaṉ puṇarntavan nāḷpōlā
īṅku nekiḻnta vaḷi.''	(tiṇimoḻi. 3)
eṉa varum.
parattimi tōṉṟa vantataṟkuc ceyyuḷ
''..........................................
kaṇaṅkoḷ iṭumaṇaṟ kāvi varuntap
piṇaṅkiru mōṭṭa tirivan taḷikkum
maṇaṅkamaḻ aimpālār ūṭali āṅkē
vaṇaṅki uṇarppāṉ tuṟi.''	(kalit. 131)
eṉṉum pāṭṭiṉuḷ tāṉ ūṭiṉāḷākavum makiḻntavāṟum avaṉvayiṟ parattimi kūṟiyavāṟum kāṇka.
iccūttirattāṟ colliyatu 'maṟintavaṟ kāṇṭal' mutalāka ōtappaṭṭa aṟuvakip poruṇmiyum, 'kippaṭṭuk kalaṅkal' mutalākak 'kūṟiyavāyil koḷḷākkāli' īṟākavarum makiḻcciyiṉāṉ maṉippaṭṭuk kalaṅkic citintavaḻi eṇṇutal cāṉṟa arumaṟiyic collutalum, ivvāṟum eṇṇantāṉ uriyāk kālattut taṉṉuyir cellumāṟu urittalum, 'vēṟṟuvarivu variṉ atu māṟṟutal' mutalākat 'tamartaṟ kātta kāraṇap pakkam' īṟākat taṉ kuṟipiḻikka niṟkap peṟum eṉṟalum, avvaḻit talivaṉ vantu peyarntuḻik kalakka miṉṟit teḷitalum, 'vaḻuviṉṟu niliiya iyaṟpaṭu poruḷ' mutalāka 'ēmam cāṉṟa uvaki' īṟākat tāṉ uriyaḷākiya neṟiyum talivaṉ ayalākiya niliyum pōla variṟ collappeṟum eṉṟalum eṉak kuṟippiṉum iṭattiṉum allatu vēṭki neṟippaṭṭavāṟākat talivikkuk kūṟṟu nikaḻumiṭam uṇarttiyavāṟu.	(21)
ituvumatu
110.	variviṭi vitta kālattu varuntiṉum
	variyā nāḷiṭi vantōṉ muṭṭiṉum
	uriyeṉat tōḻikku urittaṟ kaṇṇum
	tāṉē kūṟuṅ kālamum uḷavē.
ituvum talivimāṭṭuc col nikaḻumiṭam uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) talivaṉ iyaṟkippuṇarcci puṇarntu nīṅkukiṉṟāṉ, 'iṉṉa nāḷ varintu koḷval' eṉak kūṟit tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭattiṟku muyalātu taṇantavaḻi, yataṉit tōḻi aiyappaṭuṅ kuṟipput tōṉṟāmi maṟittoḻukiya talivi avaṉ varuntuṇiyum āṟṟātu varutta muṟiṉum, variyāta nāḷiṉkaṇ maṟintoḻukāniṉṟa talivaṉ cevili mutalāyiṉāri muṭṭiṉavaḻiyum, ''ivvoḻukkattiṉi niṉ tōḻikku uri''yeṉat talivaṉ kūṟiyavaḻiyum, talivi tāṉē kūṟuṅ kālamum uḷa e-ṟu.
ummi etirmaṟiyātalāṟ kūṟāmi perumpāṉmi. kālamum eṉṟatu ivvoḻukkam puṟattārkkup pulaṉāṅ kālam. akkālattut tōḻi matiyuṭampaṭāmal aṟivikkum e-ṟu.
iṉi, variviṭivitta kālattu varuttamuṟṟavaḻik kūṟiya ceyyuḷ:
''puṉavaṉ tuṭavip poṉpōṟ ciṟutiṉik
kaṭiyuṇ kaṭavuṭ kiṭṭa cilkural
aṟiyā tuṇṭa maññi yāṭumakaḷ
veṟiyuṟu vaṉappiṉ vērttuṟṟu naṭuṅkuñ
cūrmali nāṭaṉ kēṇmi
nīrmali kaṇṇoṭu niṉippā kiṉṟē.''	(kuṟun. 105)
eṉa varum.
variyā nāḷiṭi vantōṉ muṭṭiyavaḻit talivi kūṟiyataṟkuc ceyyuḷ:
''tāḻi kurukīṉun taṇṇan tuṟivaṉi
māḻimāṉ nōkkiṉ maṭamoḻi  nūḻi
nuḻiyu maṭamakaṉ yārkoleṉ ṟaṉṉi
puḻiyum aṭittāḷ katavu.''	(kinnili. 59)
eṉavum,
''aṟiyā miyiṉ aṉṉi añcik
kuḻiyaṉ kōtiyaṉ kuṟumpin toṭiyaṉ
viḻavayar tuṇaṅki taḻuvukañ cella
neṭunimir teruviṟ kipuku koṭumiṭi
notuma lāḷaṉ katumeṉat tākkaliṉ
kēṭpār uḷarkol illikol pōṟṟeṉa
yāṇatu pacali eṉṟaṉaṉ ataṉetir
nāṇili eluva eṉṟuvan ticiṉē
ceṟunarum viḻiyuñ cemma lōṉeṉa
naṟunutal arivi pōṟṟēṉ
ciṟumi perumiyiṟ kāṇātu tuṇintē.''	(naṟ. 50)
eṉavum varum. (itaṉkaṇ eṉṟāṉeṉa oru col varuvikka.)
uriyeṉat tōḻikku urittaṟkuc ceyyuḷ
''poṉiṇar vēṅki kaviṉiya pūmpoḻiluḷ
naṉmali nāṭaṉ nalampuṉiya  meṉmuliyāy
pōyiṉa tinnāḷ puṉattu maṟiyiṉāl
ēyiṉār iṉṟum iṉitu.''	(aintiṇiyim. 11)
eṉa varum.
iṉṉum, 'uriyeṉat tōḻik kurittaṟkaṇṇum' eṉpataṟkut talivaṟku uriyeṉat tōḻikku urittaṟkaṇṇum eṉṟumām.
u-m:
''eṉṉikol tōḻi avarkaṇṇum naṉkilli
aṉṉi mukaṉum atuvākum  poṉṉalar
puṉṉiyam pūṅkāṉaṟ cērppaṉit takkatō
niṉṉalla tilleṉ ṟuri''	(aintiṇiyeḻu. 56)
eṉa varum.	(22)
ituvumatu
111.	uyiriṉum ciṟantaṉṟu nāṇē nāṇiṉum
	ceyirtīr kāṭcik kaṟpucciṟan taṉṟeṉat
	tollōr kiḷavi pulliya neñcamoṭu
	kāmak kiḻavaṉ uḷvaḻip paṭiṉum
	tāvil naṉmoḻi kiḻavi kiḷappiṉum
	āvaki piṟavum tōṉṟumaṉ poruḷē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) uyiriṉum nāṇ ciṟantatu; ataṉiṉum kuṟṟantīrnta kāṭciyiṉiyuṭiya kaṟpuc ciṟantatu; eṉa muṉṉōr kūṟṟi yuṭkoṇṭu talivaṉuḷḷa viṭattuc cellalum varuttamillāc collit talivi collutalumākiya avvaki piṟavun tōṉṟum; avi poruḷām e-ṟu.
maṉ ākkattiṉkaṇ vantatu. eṉavē ivvāṟu ceytal poruḷalla eṉṟu kūṟaṟka e-ṟu. itaṉuḷ nāṇattiṉum kaṟpuc ciṟanta teṉṟavāṟu.
notumalar varivu nōkkik kūṟuvatu 
''aḷitō tāṉē nāṇē nammoṭu
naṉinīṭu uḻantaṉṟu maṉṉē; iṉiyē
vāṉpūṅ karumpiṉ ōṅkumaṇaṟ ciṟuciṟi
tīmpuṉal neritara vīntuk kāaṅkut
tāṅkum aḷavit tāṅkik
kāma neritarak kinnil lātē.''	(kuṟun. 149)
''kōṭī rilaṅkuvaḷi nekiḻa nāṭoṟum
pāṭila kaliḻuṅ kaṇṇoṭu pulampi
īṅkivaṇ uṟitalum uykuvam āṅkē
eḻuiṉi vāḻiyeṉ neñcē muṉāatu
kullik kaṇṇi vaṭukar muṉiyatu
palvēṟ kaṭṭi naṉṉāṭṭu umpar
moḻipeyar tēetta rāyiṉum
vaḻipaṭal cūḻnticiṉ avaruṭi nāṭṭē.''	(kuṟun. 11)
itu kāmakkiḻava ṉuḷvaḻip paṭutal.
tāvil naṉmoḻi kūṟiyataṟkuc ceyyuḷ
''maṉṟa marāatta pēemutir kaṭavuḷ
koṭiyōrt teṟūum eṉpa yāvatuṅ
koṭiyar allarem kuṉṟukeḻu nāṭar
pacii pacantaṉṟu nutalē
ñekiḻa ñekiḻntaṉṟu taṭameṉ tōḷē.''	(kuṟun. 87)
eṉa varum.	(23)
kaḷavil tōḻikūṟṟu nikaḻiṭam
112.	nāṟṟamum tōṟṟamum oḻukkamum uṇṭiyum
	ceyviṉi maṟippiṉum celaviṉum payilviṉum
	puṇarcci yetirppā ṭuḷḷuṟuttu varūum
	uṇarcci ēḻiṉum uṇarnta piṉṟi
	meyyiṉum poyyiṉum vaḻinili piḻiyātu
	palvēṟu kavarporuḷ nāṭṭat tāṉuṅ
	kuṟiyuṟaṟ ketiriya kiḻavaṉi maṟiyuṟap
	perumiyiṟ peyarppiṉum ulakurit toḻippiṉum
	arumiyiṉ akaṟciyum avaḷaṟi vuṟuttup
	piṉvā veṉṟalum pētimi yūṭṭalum
	muṉṉuṟu puṇarcci muṟiniṟut turittalum
	añciyac cuṟuttalum urittuḻik kūṭṭamoṭu
	eñcātu kiḷanta irunāṉku kiḷaviyum
	vanta kiḻavaṉi māyañ ceppip
	poṟutta kāraṇaṅ kuṟitta kāliyum
	puṇarntapiṉ avaṉvayiṉ vaṇaṅkaṟ kaṇṇuṅ
	kuṟintavaṭ paṭariṉum maṟintava ḷarukat
	taṉṉoṭum avaḷoṭum muṉṉamuṉ taḷiip
	piṉṉili nikaḻum palvēṟu maruṅkiṉum
	naṉṉayam peṟṟuḻi nayampuri iṭattiṉum
	eṇṇarum paṉṉaki kaṇṇiya vakiyiṉum
	puṇarcci vēṇṭiṉum vēṇṭāp piriviṉum
	vēḷāṇ peruneṟi vēṇṭiya iṭattiṉum
	puṇarntuḻi uṇarnta aṟimaṭac ciṟappiṉum
	ōmpaṭik kiḷavip pāṅkiṉ kaṇṇum
	ceṅkaṭu moḻiyāṉ citivuṭit tāyiṉum
	eṉpunekap pirintōḷ vaḻicceṉṟu kaṭii
	aṉputali aṭutta vaṉpuṟik kaṇṇum
	āṟṟatu tīmi aṟivuṟu kalakkamum
	kāppiṉ kaṭumi kiyaṟa variṉum
	kaḷaṉum poḻutum varinili vilakkik
	kātal mikuti uḷappaṭap piṟavum
	nāṭum ūrum illum kuṭiyum
	piṟappuñ ciṟappum iṟappa nōkki
	avaṉvayiṉ tōṉṟiya kiḷaviyoṭu tokii
	aṉinili vakiyāṉ varital vēṇṭiṉum
	aiyac ceyki tāykketir maṟuttup
	poyyeṉa māṟṟi meyvaḻik koṭuppiṉum
	avaḷvilaṅ kuṟiṉuṅ kaḷampeṟak kāṭṭiṉum
	piṟaṉvari vāyiṉum avaṉvarivu maṟuppiṉum
	muṉṉili aṟaṉeṉap paṭutaleṉ ṟiruvakip
	puritīr kiḷavi tāyiṭip pukuppiṉum
	varivuṭaṉ paṭṭōrk kaṭāval vēṇṭiṉum
	āṅkataṉ taṉmiyiṉ vaṉpuṟi uḷappaṭap
	pāṅkuṟa vanta nāleṭṭu vakiyum
	tāṅkaruñ ciṟappiṉ tōḻi mēṉa.

eṉṟatu, kaḷavoḻukkattiṉkaṇ tōḻikkuriya kiḷaviyellām tokuttu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) nāṟṟamu.... nāṭṭattāṉum eṉpatu nāṟṟamutalākac collappaṭṭa ēḻaṉāṉum puṇarccikku muntuṟṟa nilimiyi uṭkoṇṭu varum maṉanikaḻcci yēḻiṉum puṇarcciyuṇmi yaṟinta piṉṟi meyyiṉāṉum poyyiṉāṉun talivi kulattiṉuḷḷār nilimiyiṟ piḻiyātu palavāki vēṟupaṭṭa kavartta poruṇmi yiyuṭiya ārāytaṟkaṇṇum e-ṟu.
nāṟṟam eṉpatu  pūviṉāṉum cāntiṉāṉum talivaṉ māṭṭuḷatākiya kalaviyāṉ talivimāṭṭuḷatāya nāṟutal.
tōṟṟam eṉpatu  puṇarcciyāṉ varum poṟpu.
oḻukkam eṉpatu  āyattāroṭu vēṇṭiyavāṟoḻukuta liṉṟit taṉṉip pēṇiyoḻukutal.
uṇṭi eṉpatu  uṇṇum aḷaviṟ kuṟital.
ceyviṉi maṟittal āvatu  pūkkoytalum puṉalāṭalum pōlum viṉikaḷit tōḻiyi maṟittut taṉittu nikaḻttutal. aṉṟiyum talivaṉ ceyta puṇarcciyākiya karumattiṉip pulappaṭa viṭātu tōḻiyi maṟittalum eṉṟumām.
celaviṉum eṉpatu  etticiyiṉum ceṉṟu viḷiyāṭuvāḷ oru ticiyi nōkkic cēṟal.
payilviṉum eṉpatu  ōriṭattup payilutal.
puṇarcci etirppāṭu āvatu  puṇarvataṟku muntuṟṟa kālam.
uḷḷuṟuttal āvatu  uṭkōṭal.
uṇarcci ēḻāvatu  nāṟṟa mutalākac collappaṭṭavaṟṟāṉ varum maṉanikaḻcci ēḻum.
palvēṟu kavarporuḷ nāṭṭam eṉpatu  oṉṟōṭoṉṟu ovvāta vēṟupaṭṭaṉavākiya iruporuḷ payakkum coṟkaḷāṉē yārāytal.
avaṟṟuḷ cila varumāṟu:
''kaṇṇuñ cēyari parantaṉṟu nutalum
nuṇviyar poṟittu vaṇṭārk kummē
vāṅkami meṉṟōḷ maṭanti
yāṅkā yiṉaḷkol eṉṉumeṉ neñcē.''	(ciṟṟaṭṭakam)
itu talivi tōṟṟaṅkaṇṭu pāṅki kūṟiyatu. piṟavum aṉṉa.
kuṟiyuṟaṟ ketiriya kiḻavaṉi maṟiyuṟa eṉpatu  kaḷavoḻuk kattiṉkaṇṇē yuṟutaṟkākat taṉatu kuṟiyic colla vēṇṭi etirppaṭṭa talivaṉi yeṉṟavāṟu.
'maṟiyuṟa' eṉpataṉi muṉṉē kūṭṭuka.
perumiyiṟ peyarppiṉum eṉpatu  talivaṉatu perumiyāṉ nīkkalum e-ṟu.
''ivaḷē, kāṉal naṇṇiya kāmar ciṟukuṭi
nīlniṟap peruṅkaṭal kalaṅka uḷpukku
mīṉeṟi paratavar makaḷē nīyē
neṭuṅkoṭi nuṭaṅkum niyama mūtūrk
kaṭuntērc celvaṉ kātal makaṉē
niṇaccuṟā aṟutta uṇakkal vēṇṭi
iṉappuḷ ōppum emakkunalaṉ evaṉō
pulavu nāṟutum celaniṉ ṟīmō
perunīr viḷiyuḷem ciṟunal vāḻkki
nummoṭu purivatō aṉṟē
emma ṉōriṟ cemmalum uṭittē.''	(naṟ. 45)
eṉa varum.
ulakurit toḻittal eṉpatu  ulakattār makaṭkoḷḷumāṟu koḷḷeṉak kūṟutal.
''kōṭīr elvaḷik koḻumaṭaṟ kūntal
āytoṭi maṭavaral vēṇṭuti yāyiṉ
teṇkaḻic cēyiṟāp paṭūun
taṇkaṭaṟ cērppa varintaṉi koṇmō.''	(aiṅkuṟu. 196)
iṉṉum 'ulakurit toḻittal' eṉṟataṉāṟ kiyuṟi maṟiyuṅ koḷka.
''nīṭunīrk kāṉal nerunalum nittilaṅkoṇ ṭiya vantīr
kōṭuyar veṇmaṇaṟ koṟkiyem ūrivaṟṟāṟ 
kuṟiyi lēmiyām
āṭuṅ kaḻaṅkum aṇiviḷakkum ammaṉiyum 
pāṭi yavippaṉavum pantāṭap paṭuvaṉavum paṉinīr muttam.''
arumiyiṉ akaṟciyum eṉpatu  taliviyik kiṭṭutaṟku arumi kūṟi yakaṟṟutal.
u-m:
''nerunalum muṉṉā ḷelliyum oruciṟip
putuvi yākaliṉ kiḷattal nāṇi
nēriṟi vaḷittōḷniṉ tōḻi ceyta 
āruyir varuttaṅ kaḷiyā yōveṉa
eṟkuṟi yuṟuti rāyiṟ coṟkuṟi
empatat teḷiyaḷ allaḷ emakkōr
kaṭkāṇ kaṭavuḷ allaḷō peruma
vāykōṉ miḷakiṉa maliyaṅ koḻuṅkoṭi
tuñcupuli varippuṟan tivarum
mañcucūḻ maṇivari maṉṉavaṉ makaḷē.''
eṉa varum.
avaḷaṟi vuṟuttup piṉvā veṉṟalum eṉpatu  niṉṉāṟ kātalikkappaṭṭāṭkuc ceṉṟu aṟivittup piṉṉar eṉmāṭṭu vā e-ṟu.
avaṟṟuḷ, nīyē ceṉṟu aṟivi eṉṟataṟkuc ceyyuḷ
''taṉṉiyun tāṉnāṇuñ cāyalāṭ kīturippiṉ
eṉṉiyum nāṇap paṭuṅkaṇṭāy  eṉṉiya
vēyērmeṉ tōḷikku vēṟāy iṉiyorunāḷ
nīyē yurittu viṭu.''
piṉvā veṉṟaṟkuc ceyyuḷ
''nāḷvēṅki poṉviḷiyum naṉmali naṉṉāṭa
kōḷvēṅki pōṟkoṭiyar eṉṉiyaṉmār  kōḷvēṅki
aṉṉiyāl nīyum aruntaḻiyām ēlāmikku 
eṉṉiyō nāḷi eḷitu.''	(tiṇimāli. 20)
eṉa varum.
pētimi yūṭṭal eṉpatu nēriṉum avaḷ aṟivāḷorutti yallaḷ eṉṟu talivaṟkuk kūṟal.
u-m:
''naṟuntaṇ takaram vakuḷam ivaṟṟi
veṟumputalpōl vēṇṭātu veṭṭi  eṟintuḻutu
centiṉi vittuvār taṅki piṟarnōykku
nontiṉiya vallaḷō nōkku.''	(tiṇimāli. 24)
iṉṉum, pētimi yūṭṭal eṉpataṉāṉ tōḻi tāṉ aṟiyāḷ pōlak kūṟutaluṅ koḷka.
u-m:
''puṉtali mantik kallā vaṉpaṟaḻ
kuṉṟuḻi naṇṇiya muṉṟil pōkātu
eriyakin taṉṉa vītati iṇara
vēṅkiyam paṭuciṉip poruntik kiya
tēmpey tīmpāl vuvaliṟ koṭicci
eḻuteḻil citiya aḻuta kaṇṇē
tērvaṇ cōḻar kuṭanti vāyil
māriyaṅ kiṭaṅkiṉ īriya malarnta
peyaluṟu nīlam pōṉṟaṉa viralē
pāaya avvayiṟu alittaliṉ āṉātu 
āṭumaḻi tavaḻuṅ kōṭuyar potiyiṉ
ōṅkiruñ cilampiṟ pūtta
kāntaḷaṅ koḻumuki pōṉṟaṉa civantē.''	(naṟ. 379)
eṉa varum.
muṉṉuṟu puṇarcci muṟiniṟut turittalum eṉpatu  muṉṉuṟu puṇarcci muṟiyē niṟuttuk kūṟalum e-ṟu.
niṟuttuk kūṟalāvatu nīṅkaviṭātu uṭaṉpaṭṭuk kūṟal. iṉṉum, muṉpu kūṭiṉāṟ pōlak kūṭa amiyumeṉṟu kūṟutal. utāraṇam vantavaḻik kāṇka.
añci accuṟuttalum eṉpatu  tāṉ accamuṟṟu añciṉa taṉmiyit talivaṟku aṟivittalum e-ṟu. atu yāy varuvaḷeṉṟāṉum tamiyaṉmār varuva reṉṟāṉum kāvalar varuvareṉṟāṉum kūṟutal.
u-m:
''yāṉi uḻalum aṇikiḷar nīḷvarik
kāṉaka vāḻkkik kuṟavar makaḷirēm
ēṉaluḷ aiya varavu maṟ ṟeṉṉikol
kāṇiṉum kāyvar emar.''	(tiṇimoḻi. 6)
eṉa varum.
urittuḻik kūṭṭamōṭu eṉpatu  niṉṉāṟ kātalikkappaṭṭāḷ yāvaḷ eṉa viṉāyavaḻi, ittaṉmiyāḷ eṉac collak kēṭṭa tōḻi, avaḷum niṉ taṉmiyāḷ eṉa ivaṉoṭu kūṭṭi yurittalum e-ṟu.
oṭu eṇṇiṉkaṇ vantatu.
u-m:
''neṟinīr iruṅkaḻi nīlamuñ cūṭāḷ
poṟimāṇ variyalavaṉ āṭṭalum āṭṭāḷ
tirunutal vērarumpac cintiyā niṉṟāḷ
ceṟinīrttaṇ cērppayā ṉeṉcollic cērkēṉ.''
eṉa varum. 
eñcātu kiḷanta irunāṉku kiḷaviyum eṉpatu  oḻiyātu kūṟiya eṭṭuk kūṟṟum e-ṟu. muṉṉip puṇarccimuṟi yaṟintā ḷātaliṉ avaṉ irantu piṉṉiṉṟuḻi īṇṭuk kūṟiya ellām avaṉ uḷḷak karuttaṟiyun tuṇiyum taḻīikkoṇṭu kūṟiṉāḷallatu oḻittal poruḷākak kūṟāḷ eṉpatu koḷḷappaṭum. ivi eṭṭum kuṟiyuṟa vuṇartaliṉ pakuti.
vanta kiḻavaṉi māyañ ceppip poṟutta kāraṇaṅ kuṟitta kāliyum eṉpatu  māyam collivanta kiḻavaṉit talivi poṟutta kāraṇam kuṟitta kāliyum tōḻi kūṟṟu nikaḻum e-ṟu.
avvaḻit talivaṉ kuṟippum talivi kuṟippum uṇartalum talivaṉ kēṭṭataṟku māṟṟam kūṟutalum uḷavām. māyam ceppi vanta kiḻavaṉ eṉa māṟṟuka. māyam cepputalāvatu yāṉi pōntatō māṉ pōntatō eṉak kūṟal.
''irantu kuṟiyuṟātu kiḻaviyun tōḻiyum
oruṅkutalip peyta cevvi nōkkip
patiyum peyarum piṟavum viṉāayp
putuvōṉ pōlap poruntupu kiḷantu
matiyuṭam paṭuttaṟkum uriyaṉ eṉpa.''	(iṟiyaṉārakap. 6)
eṉpataṉāṟ kuṟiyuṟa vuṇartal nikaḻntuḻi itu nikaḻāteṉṟu koḷka.
ataṉkaṭ kuṟippuṇarvataṟkuc ceyyuḷ
''vēṅki malara veṟikamaḻ taṇcilampiṉ
vāṅkami meṉtōḷ kuṟavar makaḷirēm
cōrntu kuruti oḻukamaṟ ṟippuṟam
pōntatil aiya kaḷiṟu''	(tiṇimoḻi. 8)
eṉavum,
''neṭuntēr kaṭiit tamiyarāy niṉṟu
kaṭuṅkaḷiṟu kāṇīrō eṉṟīr  koṭuṅkuḻiyār
yāṉi ataruḷḷi niṟparō tampuṉattuḷ
ēṉaṟ kiḷikaṭiku vār.'' 
eṉavum,
''ēṉal kāval ivaḷum allaḷ
māṉvaḻi varukuvaṉ ivaṉum allaṉ
narantaṅ kaṇṇi ivaṉō ṭivaḷiṭik
karanta uḷḷamoṭu karutiyatu piṟitē
nammuṉ nāṇiṉar pōlat tammuḷ
matumaṟin tuṇṭōr makiḻcci pōla
uḷḷat tuḷḷē makiḻpa
collum āṭupa kaṇṇi ṉāṉē.''
eṉavum, kuṟippuṇarntu iruvaru muḷvaḻi avaṉ varavuṇartal.
puṇarntapiṉ avaṉvayiṉ vaṇaṅkaṟkaṇṇum eṉpatu  mēṟ collappaṭṭa mūvakiyāṉum puṇarcciyuṇmi poruntiyapiṉ talivaṉkaṇ tāḻa niṟṟaṟkaṇṇum e-ṟu.
atu nī karutiyatu muṭikkaṟpāli eṉavum, nī ivaḷip pātukāttal vēṇṭumeṉavum ivvaki kūṟutal.
u-m:
''............................................
araviṉ poṟiyum aṇaṅkum puṇarnta
uravuvil mēlacitta kiyi orāṅku
nirivaḷi muṉkiyeṉ tōḻiyi nōkkip
paṭukiḷi pāyum pacuṅkural ēṉal
kaṭital maṟappittā yāyiṉ iṉinī
neṭituḷḷal ōmputal vēṇṭum ivaḷē
................................................
kaṭumā kaṭavuṟūuṅ kōlpōl eṉittum
koṭumi yiliyāvatu aṟintu maṭuppal
vaḻivaḷar cāral varuṭi naṉmāṉ
kuḻavi vaḷarppavar pōlap pārāṭṭi
uḻiyiṟ piriyiṟ piriyum
iḻiyaṇi alkuleṉ tōḻiyatu kaviṉē.''	(kalit. 50)
eṉa varum.
kuṟintu avaṭ paṭariṉum eṉpatu  mēl talivaṉ puṇarcci yuṇmi yaṟintu tāḻaniṉṟa tōḻi tāṉuṅ kuṟiyuṟṟut talivimāṭṭuc cellutaṟkaṇṇuṅ kūṟṟu nikaḻum e-ṟu.
ik kiḷavi irantu piṉṉiṉṟa talivaṉ uḷḷappuṇarcciyuḷ vaḻiyum kuṟiyuṟṟu meyyuṟu puṇarcci vēṇṭit talivimāṭṭuc celluṅ kālattum okkum.
u-m:
''vaḷi aṇi muṉki vāleyiṟ ṟiṉṉaki
iḷiyar āṭun taḷiyaviḻ kāṉaṟ
kuṟuntuṟi viṉavi niṉṟa
neṭuntēr aṇṇalik kaṇṭikum yāmē.''	(aiṅkuṟu. 198)
eṉa varum.
maṟintu avaḷ arukat taṉṉoṭum avaḷoṭum muṉṉam muṉ taḷiip piṉṉili nikaḻum palvēṟu maruṅkiṉum eṉpatu  mēl talivaṉmāṭṭut tōḻi kuṟinayappikkac ceṉṟavaḻit tōḻi collum kuṟippumoḻikku avaḷ maṟittu ariyaḷākat taṉṉoṭum avaḷoṭum kuṟippiṉi muṉṉart taṭuttukkoṇṭu vaḻipaṭṭu muyalum pala vēṟu pakkattiṉkaṇṇum tōḻikūṟṟu nikaḻum e-ṟu.
maṟittalāvatu  taṉ maṉattu nikaḻcciyi oḷittal. arukuta lāvatu  icivilātārip pōla niṟṟal. muṉṉamuṉ taḷitalāvatu  kūṟṟiṉāṉaṉṟik kuṟippiṉāṉuṇarttal. mutaṉmūṉ ṟaḷii eṉṟu pāṭamāyiṉ, maṉattiṉāṉum moḻiyiṉāṉum uṭampiṉāṉum oruṅkē aḷavi eṉṟumām. piṉṉili nikaḻum palvēṟu maruṅkāvatu vaḻipāṭu koṭuvaruṅ kūṟṟu vēṟupāṭu. eṉavē talivikkut taḻiyum kaṇṇiyum koṇṭu oruvaṉ nam puṉattayal vārāniṉṟāṉ eṉavum, avaṉ eṉmāṭṭu oru kuṟiyuṭiyaṉ pōlum eṉavum, aruḷuvārkku iḵtu iṭameṉavum, avaṉ kuṟimaṟuppiṉ maṭalēṟuval eṉak kūṟip pōntāṉ piṉpu varakkaṇṭilēṉ eṉavum, innikaraṉa kūṟutal. avi varumāṟu:
''puṉipūn taḻialkul poṉ aṉṉāy cāral
tiṉikāt tiruntēmyā māka  viṉivāyttu
māviṉavu vārpōla vantavar nammāṭṭut
tāmviṉaval uṟṟatoṉ ṟuṇṭu.''	(aintiṇiyim. 14)
eṉavum,
''neyyoṭu mayakkiya uḻuntunūṟ ṟaṉṉa
vayaliyañ cilampiṉ taliyatu
ceyaliyam pakittaḻi vāṭum aṉṉāy.''	(aiṅkuṟu. 211)
eṉavum,
''ilicūḻ ceṅkāntaḷ erivāy mukiyaviḻtta īrntaṇ vāṭi
kolivēl neṭuṅkaṭ koṭicci katuppuḷaruṅ kuṉṟa nāṭaṉ
ulipaṭu vennōy uḻakkumāl antō
muliyiṭi nērpavar nērum iṭaṉitu moykuḻalē.''
eṉavum,
''puṇartuṇiyō ṭāṭum poṟiyalavaṉ nōkki
iṇartatiyum pūṅkāṉal eṉṉiyum nōkki
uṇarvoḻiyap pōṉa olitirinīrc cērppaṉ
vaṇarcuri yimpālāy vaṇṇam uṇarēṉāl.'' 	(cilap.kāṉal. 31)
eṉavum,
''taṉkuṟiyī teṉṉāṉ taḻikoṇarun taṇcilampaṉ
niṉkuṟi yeṉṉum niṉippiṉaṉāyp poṉkuṟiyum
nāḷvēṅki nīḻalum naṇṇāṉ evaṉkolō
kōḷvēṅki aṉṉāṉ kuṟippu.''	(tiṇimāli. 31)
eṉavum,
''orunāḷ vāralaṉ irunāḷ vāralaṉ
paṉṉāḷ vantu paṇimoḻi payiṟṟi
naṉṉar neñcam nekiḻtta piṉṟi
varimutir tēṉiṟ pōki yōṉē
ācāku enti yāṇṭuḷaṉ kollō
vēṟupula ṉaṉṉāṭṭup peyta
ēṟuṭi maḻiyiṟ kaliḻumeṉ neñcē.''	(kuṟun. 176)
eṉavum,
''māyōṉ aṉṉa mālvarik kavāaṉ
vāliyōṉ aṉṉa vayaṅkuveḷ aruvi
ammalik kiḻavaṉ namnayan teṉṟum
varuntiṉaṉ eṉpatōr vāyccol tēṟāy
nīyuṅ kaṇṭu numaroṭum eṇṇi
aṟivaṟin taḷaval vēṇṭu maṟutaraṟku
ariya vāḻi tōḻi periyōr
nāṭi naṭpiṉ allatu
naṭṭu nāṭārtam oṭṭiyōr tiṟattē.''	(naṟ. 32)
eṉavum varum.
naṉṉayam peṟṟuḻi nayampuri yiṭattiṉum eṉpatu  talivi kuṟi nayantami peṟṟavaḻi at talivi nayam poruntum iṭattiṉuṅ kūṟṟu nikaḻum e-ṟu.
talimakaḷ kuṟinayantami talimakaṟkuk kūṟiya ceyyuḷ
''neytaṟ parappiṟ pāvi kiṭappi
niṉkuṟi vantaṉeṉ iyaltērk koṇka
celkañ celaviyaṅ koṇmō vikalum
āral arunta vayiṟṟa
nāri mitikkum eṉmakaḷ nutalē.'' 	(kuṟun. 114)
eṉavum,
''kaṭumpulāl puṉṉi kaṭiyun tuṟiva
paṭumpulāl puḷkaṭivāṉ pukka  taṭampulām
tāḻimā ñāḻal tatintuyarnta tāḻpoḻil
ēḻimāṉ nōkki iṭam.''	(tiṇimāli. 44)
eṉavum varum.
iṉṉum nayampuri yiṭattum eṉṟataṉāṉ kaḷavoḻukkam nikaḻā niṉṟuḻik kūṟuṅ kūṟṟum īṇṭē koḷka. atu talivaṉ varumeṉavum vantā ṉeṉavuṅ kūṟutalum talimakaṉ pakaṟkuṟikkaṇ nīṅkiyavaḻik kūṟutalum eṉap palavām.
''kavarpari neṭuntēr maṇiyum icikkum
peyarpaṭa viyaṅkiya iḷiyarum olippar
kaṭalāṭu viyaliṭip polinta naṟuntaḻit
titali yalkul nalampā rāṭṭiya
varumē tōḻi vārmaṇaṟ cērppaṉ
iṟpaṭa vāṅkiya muḻavumutaṟ puṉṉi
māvari maṟikam vammati pāṉāḷ
pūviri kāṉal puṇarkuṟi vantunam
melliṇar naṟumpoḻiṟ kāṇātavaṉ
allal arumpaṭar kāṇkanām ciṟitē.'' 	(naṟ. 307)
itu varukiṉṟāṉ eṉak kūṟiyatu.
''nilavum maṟintaṉṟu iruḷum paṭṭaṉṟu
ōvat taṉṉa iṭaṉuṭi varippiṉ
pāvi aṉṉa nappuṟam kākkuñ
ciṟanta celvattu aṉṉiyun tuñciṉaḷ
keṭuttuppaṭu naṉkalam eṭuttukkoṇ ṭāṅku
naṉmārpu aṭiya muyaṅki meṉmelak
kaṇṭaṉam varukañ celmō tōḻi
kīḻu mēluṅ kāppōr nītta
vaṟuntalip peruṅkaḷiṟu pōlat
tamiyaṉ vantōṉ paṉiyali niliyē.'' 	(naṟ. 182)
itu vantāṉ eṉak kūṟiyatu.
''neytal kūmpa niḻalkuṇak koḻukak
kalcēr maṇṭilañ civantu ciṉantaṇiyap
palpūṅ kāṉalum alkiṉ ṟaṉṟē
iṉamaṇi olippap poḻutupaṭap pūṭṭi
meymmali kāmattu yāntoḻutu oḻiyat
tēruñ celpuṟam maṟiyum ūrōṭu
yāṅkā vatukol tāṉē tēmpaṭa
ūtuvaṇṭu imiruṅ kōti mārpiṉ
miṉṉivar perumpūṇ koṇkaṉoṭu
iṉṉaki mēvinām āṭiya poḻilē.''	(naṟ. 187)
itu pakaṟkuṟikkaṇ talivaṉ nīṅkiyavaḻik kūṟiyatu.
eṇṇarum paṉṉaki kaṇṇiya vakiyiṉum eṉpatu  eṇṇutaṟku ariya pala nakiyāṭṭukkaḷit talivaṉiṭam kuṟitta vakiyuṅ kūṟṟu nikaḻum e-ṟu.
aḵtu alarākumeṉṟu kūṟutal. ivvoḻukkam puṟattārkkup pulaṉāyiṉāl avar tūṟṟi nakipparākaliṉ nakiyāyiṟṟu.
u-m:
''niṟi ariyar maṉaḷiyar eṉṉātu kāmam
maṟiiṟantu maṉṟu paṭum.''	(kuṟaḷ. 1138)
''aṉṉiyum aṟintaṉaḷ alaru māyiṉṟu
naṉmaṉi neṭunakar pulampukoḷa uyirkkum
iṉṉā vāṭiyum maliyum
nummūrc celkam eḻukamō teyyō.''	(aiṅkuṟu. 238)
eṉavum varum.
puṇarcci vēṇṭiṉum eṉpatu  mēṟcollappaṭṭa pallāṟṟā ṉum talivaṟ kaṟivuṟuttavaḻip piṉṉum puṇarcci vēṇṭiṉum āṇṭut tōḻi kūṟṟu nikaḻum e-ṟu.
''neyyāl erinutuppēm eṉṟaṟṟāṟ keḷaviyāṟ
kāmam nutuppēm eṉal.''	(kuṟaḷ. 1148)

''ivaḷē, niṉcoṟ koṇṭa eṉcol tēṟip
pacunaṉi ñāḻaṟ palkiḷi oruciṟip
putunala ṉiḻanta pulampumār uṭiyaḷ
utukkāṇ teyya uḷḷal vēṇṭum
nilavum iruḷum pōlap pulavuttirik
kaṭaluṅ kāṉalun tōṉṟum
maṭaltāḻ peṇṇiyeñ ciṟunal lūrē.''	(kuṟun. 81)
itu piṉṉum puṇarcci vēṇṭiya talivaṟku iṭamuṇarttiyatu. iravu varuvāṉip pakal vāveṉṟalum pakal varuvāṉi iravu vāveṉṟaluṅ kuṟipeyarttalum ellām īṇṭē koḷka.
''ūrkkum aṇittē poyki poykikkuc
cēyttum aṉṟu ciṟukāṉ yāṟē
iritēr veṇkuruku allatu yāvatum
tuṉṉal pōkiṉṟāṟ poḻilē yāmem
kūḻik kerumaṇ koṇarkañ cēṟum 
āṇṭum varukuvaḷ perumpē tiyē.''	(kuṟun. 113)
itu pakaṟkuṟi nērntatu. āṇṭut talivikkuk kūṟumāṟu:
''oliveḷ ḷaruvi ōṅkumali nāṭaṉ
ciṟukaṭ peruṅkaḷiṟu vayappuli tākkit
tolmuraṇ cōrun tuṉṉaruñ cōli
naṭunāḷ varutalum varūum
vaṭunā ṇalamē tōḻi nāmē.''	(kuṟun. 88)
eṉa varum.
vēṇṭāp piriviṉum eṉpatu  puṇarcci vēṇṭātu pirivu vēṇṭiṉum e-ṟu.
itu talivaṉ neñciṉāṟ piriyāṉeṉpataṉāṉ vēṇṭāp piriveṉṟār. 'atu tāḷāṇ' (105) eṉpatu alarākumeṉṟu añci oruvaḻit taṇattalum oṉṟu. avvaḻit talivikku urittaṉavum talivaṟ kurittaṉavum uḷavām. avi varumāṟu:
''iṟavuppuṟat taṉṉa piṇarpaṭu taṭavumutaṟ
cuṟavukkōṭ ṭaṉṉa muḷḷilit tāḻi
peruṅkaḷiṟṟu maruppiṉ aṉṉa arumpumutirpu
naṉmāṉ uḻiyiṉ vēṟupaṭat tōṉṟi
viḻavukkaḷaṅ kamaḻum uravunīrc cērppa
iṉamaṇi neṭuntēr pākaṉ iyakkac
celīiya cēṟi yāyiṉ ivaḷē
varuvi yākiya ciṉṉāḷ
vāḻā ḷātalnaṟ kaṟintaṉi ceṉmē.''	(naṟ. 19)
itu, talivaṉ pirivu vēṇṭiyavaḻik kūṟiyatu.
''cāraṟ palaviṉ koḻuntuṇar naṟumpaḻam
iruṅkal viṭaraḷi vīḻnteṉa veṟpiṟ
peruntēṉ iṟāloṭu citaṟu nāṭaṉ
pēramar maḻikkaṇ kaliḻattaṉ
cīruṭi naṉṉāṭṭuc cellum aṉṉāy.''	(aiṅkuṟu. 214)
itu talivikku urittatu.
''kāṉalam peruntuṟik kalitiri tiḷikkum
vāṉuyar eṟimaṇal ēṟi āṉātu
kāṇkam vammō tōḻi
ceṟivaḷi nekiḻttōṉ eṟikaṭal nāṭē.''	(aiṅkuṟu. 199)
itu taliviyi āṟṟuvittatu.
''iruḷtiṇin taṉṉa īrntaṇ koḻuniḻal
nilavuk kuvittaṉṉa veṇmaṇa loruciṟik
karuṅkōṭṭup puṉṉip pūmpoḻil pulampa
iṉṉum vārār varūum
palmīṉ vēṭṭat teṉṉiyar timilē.''	(kuṟun. 123)
eṉavum varum.
vēḷāṇ peruneṟi vēṇṭiya viṭattiṉum eṉpatu vēḷāṇmiyāvatu upakāram. peruneṟiyāvatu upakāramākiya peruneṟi eṉka. ataṉit tōḻi talivaṉi vēṇṭik kōṭaṟ kaṇṇum e-ṟu.
''neṭuvēḷ mārpiṉ āram pōlac
cevvāy vāṉan tīṇṭimīṉ aruntum
piṅkāṟ kokkiṉ niripaṟi yukappa
elli pippayak kaḻippik kuṭavayiṉ
kalcērn taṉṟē palkatir ñāyiṟu
matareḻil maḻikkaṇ kaliḻa ivaḷē
perunāṇ aṇinta ciṟumeṉ cāyal
māṇalañ citiya ēṅki āṉātu
aḻaltoṭaṅ kiṉaḷē peruma ataṉāṟ
kaḻiccuṟā veṟinta puṭṭāḷ attiri
neṭunīr iruṅkaḻip parimelin tacii
valvil iḷiyaro ṭellic cellātu
cērntaṉi celiṉē citikuva tuṇṭō
peṇṇi ōṅkiya veṇmaṇal paṭappi
aṉṟil akavum āṅkaṇ
ciṟukural neytalem peruṅkaḻi nāṭṭē.''	(akam. 120)
eṉpatum,
''nilāviṉ ilaṅkum'' eṉṉum akappāṭṭiṉuḷ,
''cērntaṉir celkuvi rāyiṉ yāmum
emvari aḷaviyiṟ peṭkuvam
nummop patuvō uritticiṉ emakkē.''	(akam. 200)
eṉpatum koḷka.
itaṉāṟ payaṉ illaṟam naṭattal vēṇṭum eṉpatu.
puṇarntuḻi yuṇarnta aṟimaṭac ciṟappiṉum eṉpatu  taliva ṉoṭu talivi puṇarntavaḻi āṇṭup poruntiya aṟivu maṭampaṭṭa ciṟappiṉkaṇṇum e-ṟu.
aḵtāvatu alla kuṟippaṭutal. avvaḻiyum tōḻi kūṟṟu nikaḻum.
''koṭumuḷ maṭaltāḻik kūmpaviḻnta oṇpū
iṭiyuḷ iḻutoppat tōṉṟip  puṭiyelān
teyvam kamaḻun teḷikaṭal taṇcērppaṉ
ceytāṉ teḷiyāk kuṟi.''	(aintiṇiyim. 49)
''iṭumaṇal ekkar akaṉkāṉal cērppaṉ
kaṭumāṉ maṇiyaravam eṉṟu  koṭuṅkuḻi
puḷḷaravaṅ kēṭṭup peyarntāḷ ciṟukuṭiyar
uḷḷaravam nāṇuvar eṉṟu.	(aintiṇiyeḻu. 59)
''amma vāḻiyō aṉṉinam paṭappi
miṉṉēr nuṭaṅkiṭic ciṉṉiḻa lākiya
puṉṉi meṉkāy poruciṉi ariya
vāṭuvaḷi tūkkiya aciviṟ kollō
teṇṇīr poykiyuḷ vīḻnteṉa
eṇṇiṉi yurimō uṇarkuval yāṉē.''
eṉa varum.
ōmpaṭik kiḷavip pāṅkiṉ kaṇṇum eṉpatu  ōmpaṭuttutaṟ poruṭpakutikkaṇṇuṅ kūṟṟu nikaḻum e-ṟu.
aḵtāvatu oruvaḻittaṇakkumvaḻi ōmpaṭi kūṟutal.
''perunaṉ ṟāṟṟiṟ pēṇārum uḷarē
orunaṉ ṟuṭiyā ḷāyiṉum purimāṇṭu
pulavi tīra vaḷimati yilikavar
pāṭami yoḻukiya taṇṇaṟuñ cāral
meṉṉaṭi mariyā tuñcum
naṉmali nāṭa niṉṉala tilaḷē.''	(kuṟun. 115)
eṉavum,
''eṟintemar tāmuḻuta īrṅkural ēṉal
maṟantuṅ kiḷiyiṉamum vārā  kaṟaṅkaruvi
māmali nāṭa maṭamoḻi taṉkēṇmi
nīmaṟaval neñcattuk koṇṭu'' 	(aintiṇiyim. 18)
eṉavum varum.
itaṉuḷ kiḷikaṭiya yām vārēm nī maṟavātoḻital vēṇṭu meṉṟavāṟu.
ceṅkaṭu moḻiyāṟ citivuṭittāyiṉu meṉpu nekap pirintōḷ vaḻicceṉṟu kaṭii aṉputali yaṭutta vaṉpuṟik kaṇṇum eṉpatu  cevviya kaṭiya colliṉāṉē talivaṉ aṉpu citivuṭittāyiṉum eṉpurukumāṟu piriyappaṭṭavaḷiṭattuc ceṉṟu talivaṉ aṉpuṭimiyiṉ aḷippaṉ eṉa āṟṟuvitta vaṟpuṟuttaṟ kaṇṇun tōḻikūṟṟu nikaḻum e-ṟu.
ceṅkaṭumoḻi eṉṟatu  koṭiya kaṭumoḻiyē yaṉṟi maṉatti ṉāṉ iṉiyaḷākik kūṟum kaṭumoḻi. aḵtāvatu iyaṟpaḻittal. avvāṟu iyaṟpaḻittavaḻit talivaṉ aṉpu citivuṭittāyiṉum e-ṟu. aṉpu taliyaṭutta vaṉpuṟiyāvatu talivaṉ iṉṟi yamiyāṉ eṉa āṟṟavittal.
iyaṟpaḻittaṟkuc ceyyuḷ
''mācaṟak kaḻīiya yāṉi pōlap
perumpeyal uḻanta irumpiṇart tuṟukal
pital orutali cēkku nāṭaṉnīṭu
nōytan taṉaṉē tōḻi
pacali ārntaṉa kuvaḷiyaṅ kaṇṇē.''	(kuṟun. 13)
vaṉpuṟikkuc ceyyuḷ
''makiḻnaṉ mārpē veyyi eṉanī
aḻiyal vāḻi tōḻi naṉṉaṉ
naṟumā koṉṟu nāṭṭiṟ pōkiya
oṉṟumoḻik kōcar pōla
vaṉkaṭ cūḻcciyum vēṇṭumāṟ ciṟitē.''	(kuṟun. 73)
''meyyil tīrā mēvaru kāmamōṭu
eyyāy āyiṉum urippal tōḻi
koyyā muṉṉuṅ kuralvārpu tiṉiyē
aruvi āṉṟa piṅkāl tōṟum
iruvi tōṉṟiṉa palavē nīyē
murukumuraṇ koḷḷun tēmpāy kaṇṇip
pariyal nāyoṭu paṉmalip paṭarum
vēṭṭuvaṟ peṟalō ṭamintaṉi yāḻaniṉ
pūkkeḻu toṭali nuṭaṅka veḻunteḻuntu
kiḷḷit teḷviḷi yiṭiyiṭi payiṟṟi
āṅkāṅ koḻukā yāyiṉ aṉṉi
ciṟukiḷi kaṭital tēṟṟāḷ ivaḷeṉap
piṟarttantu niṟukkuva ḷāyiṉ
uṟaṟkari tākumavaṉ malarnta mārpē.''	(akam. 28)
āṟṟuvittaṟkuc ceyyuḷ
''kuṟuṅki irumpuli kōḷval ēṟṟi
neṭumputaṟ kāṉattu maṭappiṭi īṉṟa
naṭuṅkunaṭik kuḻavi koḷīiya palaviṉ
paḻantūṅku koḻuniḻal oḷikku nāṭaṟkuk
koytiṭu taḷiriṉ vāṭiniṉ
meypiṟi tākal evaṉkol aṉṉāy.''	(aiṅkuṟu. 216)
''aḻiyal āyiḻi aṉpuperi tuṭiyaṉ
paḻiyum añcum payamali nāṭaṉ
nillā miyē niliyiṟ ṟākaliṉ
nallici vēṭṭa nayaṉuṭiya neñciṟ
kaṭappāṭ ṭāḷaṉ uṭipporuḷ pōlat
taṅkutaṟ kuriya taṉṟuniṉ
aṅkaluḻ mēṉip pāaya pacappē.''	(kuṟun. 143)
eṉavum,
''peruṅki iruṅkaḷiṟu aivaṉam māntik
karuṅkāl marāmpoḻiṟ pācaṭit tuñcuñ
curumpimir cōli malināṭaṉ kēṇmi
poruntiṉārk kēmāp puṭittu.''	(aintiṇiyeḻu. 12)
eṉavum varum.
āṟṟatu tīmi aṟivuṟu kalakkamum... varital vēṇṭiyum eṉpatu  talivaṉ varuneṟiyiṉatu tīmiyit tāṅkaḷ aṟivuṟṟataṉāṉ eytiya kalakkattāṉum kāvaṟ kaṭumi variyiṟantataṉāṉum kuṟiyiṭamum kālamumākat tāṅkaḷ varinta nilimiyi vilakkit talivi kātal mikutal uṭpaṭap piṟavun talivaṉatu nāṭum ūrum illum kuṭiyum piṟappum ciṟappum mikutiyum nōkkit talivaṉ māṭṭuk kiḷakkuṅ kiḷaviyōṭē kūṭa attaṉmittākiya nilavakiyiṉāṉē varital vēṇṭiyum tōḻi kūṟum e-ṟu. avaṟṟuḷ, 
āṟiṉṉāmi kūṟiyataṟkuc ceyyuḷ
''cāraṟ puṉatta peruṅkuraṟ ciṟutiṉip
pēramar maḻikkaṇ koṭicci kaṭiyavuñ
cōlic ciṟukiḷi uṇṇum nāṭa
āriruḷ perukiṉa vāral
kōṭṭumā vaḻaṅkuṅ kāṭṭaka neṟiyē.''	(aiṅkuṟu. 282)
kāppu vari yiṟantataṟkuc ceyyuḷ
''pallōr tuñcu naḷeṉ yāmattu
uravukkaḷiṟu pōlvan tiravukkatava muyaṟal
kēḷēm allēṅ kēṭṭaṉam peruma
ōri muruṅkap pīli cāya
naṉmayil valippaṭ ṭāṅkuyām
uyaṅkutoṟu muyaṅkum aṟaṉil yāyē.''	(kuṟun. 244)
eṉavum,
''karuṅkaṇ tākkali perumpiṟi tuṟṟeṉak
kimmi uyyāk kāmar manti
kallā vaṉpaṟaḻ kiḷimutaṟ cērtti
ōṅkuvari yaṭukkattup pāyntuyir cekukkuñ
cāral nāṭa naṭunāḷ
vāral vāḻiyō varuntutum yāmē.''	(kuṟun. 69)
eṉavum varum.
kātaṉ mikuti kūṟiyataṟkuc ceyyuḷ
''vēral vēli vērkkōṭ palaviṉ
cāral nāṭa cevviyi ākumati
yāraḵ taṟintici ṉōrē cāraṟ
ciṟukōṭṭup perumpaḻan tūṅki yāṅkivaḷ
uyirtavac ciṟitu kāmamō peritē.''	(kuṟun. 18)
'piṟavum' eṉṟataṉāṉ talivaṉip paḻittaluṅ koḷka.
''neṭuvari miciyatu kuṟuṅkāl varuṭi
tiṉipāy kiḷḷi veruvum nāṭa
vallimaṉ ṟamma poyttal
vallāy maṉṟanī allatu ceyalē.''	(aiṅkuṟu. 287)
itu, talivaṉip paḻittatu.
''kuṉṟak kuṟavaṉ kātal maṭamakaḷ
meṉtōṭ koṭicciyip peṟaṟkaritu tilla
pimpuṟap paṭukiḷi yōppavavar
puṉpula mayakkattu viḷintaṉa tiṉiyē.''	(aiṅkuṟu. 260)
itu, puṉakkāvaliṉi iṉṟeṉṟatu.
''koṭicci yiṉkural kiḷicet taṭukkattup
piṅkural ēṉaṟ paṭartaruṅ kiḷiyeṉak
kāvaluṅ kaṭiyunar pōlvar
māmali nāṭa varintaṉi koṇmē.''	(aiṅkuṟu. 289)
itu, kuṟavariyalpu uṇartti varika veṉṟatu.
''veṟikamaḻ veṟpaṉeṉ meynnīrmi koṇṭa(tu)
aṟiyāḷmaṟ ṟaṉṉō aṇaṅkaṇaṅkiṟ ṟeṉṟu
maṟiyīrt tutirantūy vēlaṟ ṟarīi
veṟiyō ṭalamvarum yāy.''	(aintiṇiyim. 20)
itu, veṟi yaccuṟuttiyatu.
''iṉamī ṉiruṅkaḻi ōtam ulāva
maṇinīr parakkun tuṟiva takumō
kuṇanīrmi kuṉṟāk koṭiyaṉṉāḷ pakkam
niṉinīrmi illā oḻivu''	(tiṇimoḻi. 44)
iḵtaruḷal vēṇṭumeṉṟatu.
iṉṉum 'piṟavum' eṉṟataṉāṉ talimakaḷ taṉṉi yaḻintami kūṟutalun talivaṉmāṭṭu varumiṭiyūṟu añcutaluṅ koḷka. atu varumāṟu:
''..................................................
taṉevvaṅ kūriṉum nīceyta aruḷiṉmi
eṉṉiyu maṟittāḷ eṉtōḻi atukēṭṭu
niṉṉiyāṉ piṟarmuṉṉarp paḻikūṟal tāṉnāṇi''	(kali. 44)
itu, talimakaḷ taṉṉi aḻintataṟkaṇ vantatu.
''kariporu kāṉyāṟṟaṅ kallata remmuḷḷi varuti rāyiṉ
ariyiruḷ yāmat taṭupuliyō  nummañci yakaṉṟu pōka
nariyurumē ṟuṅkivēl añcuka nummi
variyara maṅkiyar vavvuta lañcutum vāra liyō.''
iḵtu avaṉūṟu añcutaṟkaṇ vantatu.
nāṭumutalāyiṉa cuṭṭit talivaṉmāṭṭut tōṉṟuṅ kiḷaviyāvaṉa  nī ittaṉmiyākiya nāṭṭiyuṭiyi; ittaṉmiyākiya nakariyuṭiyi; ittaṉmittākiya illi yuṭiyi; ittaṉmittākiya kuṭippiṟappi yuṭiyi; ittaṉmittākiya ciṟappuṭiyi eṉa avaṟṟiṉ mikutipaṭak kūṟutal. avi varumāṟu:
''kōḻili vāḻi'' eṉṉum neṭuntokip pāṭṭiṉuḷ (2),
''kuṟiyā viṉpam eḷitiṉ niṉmalip
palvēṟu vilaṅkum eytu nāṭa
kuṟitta iṉpam niṉakkevaṉ ariya.''
eṉa nāṭu cuṭṭi vantatu.
''kāmaṅ kaṭappa uḷḷam iṉippa
yāmvantu kāṇpatōr paruva māyiṉ
ōṅkit tōṉṟum uyarvarikku
yāṅkeṉap paṭuvatu nummūr teyyō.''	(aiṅkuṟu. 237)
iḵtu ūrpaṟṟi vantatu.
''tuṇipuṇarn teḻutarum'' eṉṉum kalittokip pāṭṭiṉuḷ (135),
''kaṭimalarp puṉṉikkīḻk kāriki tōṟṟāḷit
toṭinekiḻnta tōḷaḷāt tuṟappāyāl maṟṟuniṉ
kuṭimikkaṭ periyatōr kuṟṟamāyk kiṭavātō.''
itu, kuṭimi paṟṟi vantatu.
''āymalarp puṉṉikkīḻ aṇinalan tōṟṟāḷi
nōymali niliyaḷāt tuṟappāyāl maṟṟuniṉ
vāymikkaṇ periyatōr vañcamāyk kiṭavātō.''
''tikaḻmalarp puṉṉikkīḻt tirunalan tōṟṟāḷi
ikaḻmalark kaṇṇaḷāt tuṟappāyāl maṟṟuniṉ
pukaḻmikkaṭ periyatōr pukarākik kiṭavātō.''	(kalit. 135)
''.......................................................
tāmarik kaṇṇiyit taṇṇaṟuñ cāntiṉi
nēritaḻk kōtiyāḷ ceykuṟi nīvariṉ
maṇaṅkamaḻ nāṟṟatta maliniṉṟu palipeṟūum
aṇaṅkeṉa añcuvar ciṟukuṭi yōrē.''	(kalit. 52)
piṟavum aṉṉa.
aiyac ceyki tāyk ketirmaṟuttup poyyeṉa māṟṟi meyvaḻik koṭuppiṉum eṉpatu  talivikkup piṟaroṭu kūṭṭa muṇṭeṉac colli aiyuṟṟavaḻi ataṉi maṟuttut talivi ceyta ceykiyip poyyeṉa nīkkip piṟitōr āṟṟāṉ meyvaḻikkaṭ paṭuttiṉum e-ṟu.
aḵtāmāṟu talivi kuṟivaḻic celkiṉṟataṉik kāṇṭal.
''urumuraṟu karuviya perumaḻi taliip
peyalāṉ ṟavinta tūṅkiruḷ naṭunāḷ
miṉṉunimirn taṉṉa kaṉaṅkuḻi imippap
piṉṉuviṭu neṟiyiṟ kiḷiiya kūntalaḷ
variyiḻi mayiliṉ olkuvaṉaḷ otuṅki
miṭiyūr piḻiyak kaṇṭaṉeṉ ivaḷeṉa
aliyal vāḻivēṇ ṭaṉṉinam paṭappic
cūruṭic cilampiṟ cuṭarppū vēyntu
tāmvēṇ ṭuruviṉ aṇaṅkumār varumē
naṉaviṉ vāyē pōlat tuñcunark
kaṉavāṇṭu maruṭṭalum uṇṭē ivaḷtāṉ
cuṭariṉṟu tamiyaḷum paṉikkum veruvara
maṉṟa marātta kūki kuḻaṟiṉum
neñcaḻin taraṇañ cēru mataṉṟalip
pulikkaṇat taṉṉa nāytoṭar viṭṭu
murukaṉ aṉṉa cīṟṟattuk kaṭuntiṟal
entiyum illa ṉāka
añcuvaḷ allaḷō ivaḷatu ceyalē.''	(akam. 158)
eṉa varum.
''vēṅki naṟumalar veṟpiṭi yāmkoytu
māntaḷir mēṉi viyarppamaṟ  ṟāṅkeṉittum
pāyntaruvi āṭiṉēm ākap paṇimoḻikkuc
cēntaṉavāñ cēyarikkaṇ tām.''	(aintiṇiyim. 15)
eṉpatum atu.
avaḷvilaṅkuṟiṉum.... tāyiṭippukuppiṉum eṉpatu  talivi kāppu mikutiyāṉuṅ kātaṉ mikutiyāṉum notumalar variviṉāṉum tamarvarivu maṟuttataṉāṉum vēṟupaṭṭavaḻi, iḵtu eṟṟiṉāṉ āyiṟṟu eṉac cevili aṟivari viṉāayk kuṟipārkkum iṭattiṉum, aḵtaṉṟi veṟiyāṭṭiṭattiṉum, piṟar varivu vantuḻiyum, avar varivu maṟuttavaḻiyum, muṉṉili vakiyāṉātal aṟattoṭu nilivakiyāṉātal ivviru vakiyāṉun, talivaṟkum talivikkum taṉakkuṅ kulattiṟkum kuṟṟan tīrnta kiḷaviyit tāymāṭṭup pukuta viṭuttalum e-ṟu.
pukutaviṭuttalāvatu nirampac collātu tōṟṟuvāy ceytal. annālvakip poruḷiṉum muṉṉilik kiḷavi varumāṟu:
''poypaṭu paṟiyāk kaḻaṅkē meyyē
maṇivarik kaṭci maṭamayil ālum
malarnta vaḷḷiyaṅ kāṉaṅ kiḻavōṉ
āṇṭaki viṟalvēḷ allaṉ ivaḷ
pūṇtāṅ kiḷamuli aṇaṅki yōṉē.''	(aiṅkuṟu. 250)
itu, kuṟipārttavaḻik kūṟiyatu, kaḻaṅku muṉṉiliyāka.
''amma vāḻi tōḻi paṉmalar
naṟuntaṇ cōli nāṭukeḻu neṭuntaki
kuṉṟam pāṭā ṉāyiṉ
eṉpayañ ceyumō vēlaṟku veṟiyē.''	(aiṅkuṟu. 244)
itu, taliviyi muṉṉiliyākak kūṟiyatu.
''neytal naṟumalarc ceruntiyoṭu viriik
kipuṉi naṟuntār kamaḻu mārpaṉ
aruntiṟaṟ kaṭavu ḷallaṉ
peruntuṟik kaṇṭivaḷ aṇaṅki yōṉē.''	(aiṅkuṟu. 182)
itu, vēlaṉi muṉṉiliyākak kūṟiyatu.
''kaṭavuṭ kaṟcuṉi aṭiyiṟan taviḻnta
paṟiyāk kuvaḷi malaroṭu kāntaḷ
kuruti oṇpū urukeḻak kaṭṭip
peruvari aṭukkam poṟpac cūrmakaḷ
aruvi iṉṉiyat tāṭu nāṭaṉ
mārputara vanta paṭarmali yarunōy
niṉṉaṇaṅ kaṉmi yaṟintum aṇṇāntu
kārnaṟuṅ kaṭampiṉ kaṇṇi cūṭi
vēlaṉ vēṇṭa veṟimaṉi vantōy
kaṭavu ḷāyiṉum āka
maṭavi maṉṟa vāḻiya murukē.''	(naṟ. 34)
itu, murukaṉi muṉṉiliyākak kūṟiyatu. piṟavumaṉṉa.
''aṉṉi vāḻivēṇ ṭaṉṉi muḻaṅkukaṭal
tiritaru muttam veṇmaṇal imikkun
taṇṇan tuṟivaṉ vanteṉap
poṉṉiṉuñ ciṟantaṉṟu kaṇṭiciṉ nutalē.''	(aiṅkuṟu. 105)
itu, muṉṉilippakuti; notumalar varivupaṟṟi vantatu.
''kuṉṟak kuṟavaṉ kātal maṭamakaḷ
aṇimayi laṉṉa acinaṭik koṭicciyip
peruvari nāṭaṉ variyu mākil
koṭuttaṉem āyiṉō naṉṟē
iṉṉum āṉātu naṉṉutal tuyarē.''	(aiṅkuṟu. 258)
iḵtu, avaṉ varivu maṟuttuḻik kūṟiyatu.
iṉi, aṟattoṭunilip pakuti eḻuvakippaṭum. avi yāmāṟu:
''eḷittal ēttal vēṭki yurittal
kūṟutal ucāvutal ētīṭu talippāṭu
uṇmi ceppuṅ kiḷaviyoṭu tokii
avveḻu vakiya eṉmaṉār pulavar''	(poruḷi. 12)
eṉap poruḷiyaluṭ kūṟiya cūttirattāṉē koḷka.
eḷittal eṉpatu  talivaṉ nammāṭṭu eḷiya ṉeṉṟu kūṟutal. ataṉatu payam makaḷuṭit tāyar tamvaḻi oḻukuvārkku makaṭkoṭi vēṇṭuva rātalāṉ, eḷiyaṉeṉpatu kūṟi aṟattoṭu niṟkap peṟu meṉṟavāṟu.
''aṉṉi aṟiyiṉum aṟika alarvāy
ammeṉ cēri kēṭpiṉuṅ kēṭka
piṟitoṉ ṟiṉmi aṟiyak kūṟik
koṭuñcuḻip pukāart teyva nōkkik
kaṭuñcūḷ tarukuval niṉakkē kāṉal
toṭali āyamoṭu kaṭaluṭaṉ āṭiyuñ
ciṟṟil iḻittuñ ciṟucōṟu kuviiyum
varuntiya varuttan tīra yāmciṟitu
iruntaṉa māka eyta vantu
taṭameṉ paṇittōḷ maṭanal līrē
ellum elliṉ ṟacivumika uṭiyēṉ 
mellilip parappiṉ viruntuṇ ṭiyāṉumik
kalleṉ ciṟukuṭit taṅkiṉmaṟ ṟevaṉō
eṉamoḻin taṉaṉē oruvaṉ avaṟkaṇṭu
iṟiñciya mukattem puṟañcērpu porunti
ivinumak kuriya alla iḻinta
koḻumīṉ valci eṉṟaṉam iḻumeṉa
neṭuṅkoṭi nuṭaṅku nāvāy tōṉṟuva
kāṇā mōveṉak kāliṟ citiyā
nillātu peyarnta pallō ruḷḷum
eṉṉē kuṟitta nōkkamoṭu naṉṉutāl
oḻikō yāṉeṉa aḻitakak kūṟi
yāṉpeyar keṉṉa nōkkit tāṉtaṉ
neṭuntērk koṭiñci paṟṟi
niṉṟōṉ pōlum iṉṟumeṉ kaṭkē.''	(akam. 110)
eṉa varum. aṉṉi eṉṟatu naṟṟāyi.
ēttal eṉpatu  talivaṉi uyarttuk kūṟutal. atu, makaḷuṭit tāyar 'talivaṉ uyarntāṉ' eṉṟavaḻi maṉamakiḻvarākaliṉ, avvāṟu kūṟappaṭṭatu. uyarttuk kūṟi aṟattoṭu niṟkappeṟum e-ṟu.
''aṉṉāy vāḻivēṇ ṭaṉṉi niṉmakaḷ
pālum uṇṇāḷ paḻaṅkaṇ koṇṭu
naṉipacan taṉaḷeṉa viṉavuti ataṉtiṟam
yāṉun teṟṟeṉa uṇarēṉ mēṉāḷ
malipūñ cāralem tōḻi mārōṭu
oliciṉi vēṅki koykuvañ ceṉṟuḻip
pulipuli eṉṉum pūcal tōṉṟa
oṇceṅ kaḻunīrk kaṇpōl āyitaḻ
ūci pōkiya cūḻcey māliyaṉ
pakkañ cērttiya ceccik kaṇṇiyaṉ
kuyamaṇ ṭākañ ceñcāntu nīvi
varipuṉi villaṉ orukaṇi terintukoṇṭu
yātō maṟṟam mātiṟam paṭareṉa
viṉavi niṟṟan tōṉē avaṟkaṇṭu
emmuḷ emmuḷ meymmaṟi poṭuṅki
nāṇi niṉṟaṉe mākap pēṇi
aivaki vakutta kūntal āynutal
miyīr ōti maṭavīr numvāyp
poyyum uḷavō eṉṟaṉaṉ piyeṉap
parimuṭuku tavirtta tēraṉ etirmaṟuttu
niṉmakaḷ uṇkaṇ paṉmā ṇōkkic
ceṉṟōṉ maṉṟaak kuṉṟukiḻa vōṉē
pakalmāy antip paṭucuṭar amiyattu
avaṉmaṟi tēem nōkki maṟṟivaṉ
makaṉē tōḻi eṉṟaṉaḷ
ataṉaḷa vuṇṭukōḷ matival lōrkkē.''	(akam. 48)
itaṉuḷ kaḻunīr māliyaṉ, veṭcik kaṇṇiyaṉ eṉak kūṟiṉamiyāṉ, avaṉ nāṭṭiṟkum malikkun talivaṉ eṉpatu paṭavum, orukaṇi terintukoṇṭu puli yāteṉṟa avaṉatu vīriyamuṅ kūṟi uyarttavāṟuṅ kāṇka.
vēṭkiyurittalāvatu  talivaṉmāṭṭut talivi vēṭki yum talivimāṭṭut talivaṉ vēṭkiyum kūṟutal. vēṭkikūṟi aṟattoṭu niṟkum e-ṟu.
''niṉmaka ḷuṇkaṇ paṉmā ṇōkkic ceṉṟōṉ'' eṉpatu talivaṉ vēṭki kūṟiyavāṟām.
''aṉṉāy vāḻivēṇ ṭaṉṉi eṉtōḻi
naṉinā ṇuṭiyaḷ niṉṉum añcum
oliveḷ ḷaruvi yōṅkumali nāṭaṉ
malarnta mārpiṟ pāyal
tavanaṉi veyyaḷ nōkō yāṉē.''	(aiṅkuṟu. 205)
itu, talivi vēṭki kūṟiyatu.
kūṟutalāvatu  taliviyit talivaṟkuk koṭukka vēṇṭu meṉpatu paṭak kūṟutal.
u-m:
''vāṭāta cāṉṟōr varavetir koṇṭirāyk
kōṭātu nīrkoṭuppiṉ allatu  vāṭā
eḻilum muliyum iraṇṭiṟku munnīrp
poḻilum viliyāmō pōntu.''	(tiṇimāli. 15)
eṉa varum.
''kaṟivaḷar cilampiṟ kaṭavuṭ pēṇi
aṟiyā vēlaṉ veṟiyeṉak kūṟum
atumaṉaṅ koḷkuvi aṉṉiyivaḷ
putumalar maḻikkaṇ pulampiya nōykkē.''	(aiṅkuṟu. 243)
eṉavum varum.
ucāvutal eṉpatu  veṟiyāṭṭuṅ kaḻaṅkum iṭṭurittuḻi vēlaṉōṭātal piṟarōṭātal tōḻi ucāvutal.
''murukayarntu vanta mutuvāy vēla
ciṉaval ōmpumati viṉavuva tuṭiyēṉ
palvē ṟuruviṟ cillaviḻ maṭiyoṭu
ciṟumaṟi koṉṟivaḷ naṟunutal nīvi
vaṇaṅkiṉi koṭutti yāyiṉ aṇaṅkiya
viṇtōy māmalic cilampaṉ
taṇṭā rakalamum uṇṇumō paliyē.''	(kuṟun. 362)
itu, vēlaṉoṭu ucāvutal.
''iṉṟiyāṇ ṭiyaṉō tōḻi kuṉṟattup
paḻaṅkuḻi akaḻnta kāṉavaṉ kiḻaṅkiṉōṭu
kaṇṇakaṉ tūumaṇi peṟūu nāṭaṉ
aṟivukāḻk koḷḷum aḷavic ceṟitoṭi
emmil varukuvi nīyeṉap
pommal ōti nīvi yōṉē.''	(kuṟun. 379)
itu cevili kēṭpat taliviyoṭu tōḻi ucāviyatu. piṟavumaṉṉa. 
ētīṭu talippāṭu eṉpatu  yātāṉumōr ētuvi iṭiyiṭṭuk koṇṭu talippaṭṭami kūṟutal.
u-m:
''kāmar kaṭumpuṉal kalantemmō ṭāṭuvāḷ
.............................................................
arumaḻi taralvēṇṭil tarukiṟkum perumiyaḷē.''	(kalit. 39)
itu, puṉaliṭi utaviṉāṉeṉat talippāṭu kūṟiyatu.
''cuḷḷi cuṉinīlañ cōpā likiceyali
aḷḷi aḷakattiṉ mēlāyntu  teḷḷi
itaṇāṟ kaṭiyoṭuṅkā īrṅkaṭā yāṉi
utaṇāṟ kaṭintāṉ uḷaṉ.'' 	(tiṇimālinūṟ. 2)
itu, kaḷiṟṟiṭi utaviṉāṉeṉat talippāṭu kūṟiyatu.
''aṉṉāy vāḻivēṇ ṭaṉṉi eṉṉi
tāṉu malintāṉ emakkuntaḻi yāyiṉa
poṉvī maṇiarum piṉavē
eṉṉa maraṅkolavar cāra lavvē.''	(aiṅkuṟu. 201)
itu, taḻiyum kaṇṇiyun tantāṉeṉpatupaṭak kūṟiyatu.
uṇmi ceppum kiḷavi yāvatu  paṭṭāṅku kūṟutal.
''alkaṉmaḻi poḻinta akaṉkaṇ aruvi
āṭukaḻi aṭukkat tiḻitaru nāṭaṉ
peruvari aṉṉa tiruviṟal viyaṉmārpu
muyaṅkātu kaḻinta nāḷivaḷ
mayaṅkitaḻ maḻikkaṇ kaliḻum aṉṉāy.''	(aiṅkuṟu. 220)
ivvaki yellām tattaṅ kuṭimik kēṟṟavaḻik koḷka.
varivuṭaṉ paṭṭōrk kaṭāval vēṇṭiyum eṉpatu  tamar varivuṭaṉpaṭṭamiyit talivaṟku urikka vēṇṭiyum e-ṟu.
utāraṇam vantavaḻik kāṇka.
āṅkataṉ ṟaṉmiyiṉ vaṉpuṟi eṉpatu  avvāṟu varivuṭam paṭṭa taṉmiyiṉāṉ taliviyi vaṟpuṟuttaṟkaṇṇum e-ṟu.
''kūrmuḷ muṇṭakak kūrmpaṉi māmalar
nūlaṟu muttiṟ kāloṭu pāṟit
tuṟitoṟum parakkun tūmaṇaṟ cērppaṉi
yāṉuṅ kātaleṉ yāyumnaṉi veyyaḷ
entiyuṅ koṭīiyar vēṇṭum
ampal ūrum avaṉoṭu moḻimē.''	(kuṟun. 51)
''amma vāḻi tōḻi nammoṭu
ciṟutiṉik kāvala ṉākip perituniṉ
meṉtōḷ nekiḻavum tirunutal pacappavum
poṉpōl viṟaṟkaviṉ tolitta
kuṉṟa nāṭaṟ kayarntaṉar maṇaṉē.''	(aiṅkuṟu. 230)
eṉa varum.
pāṅkuṟa vanta nāleṭṭu vakiyum eṉpatu  pakutippaṭa vanta muppattiraṇṭu vakippaṭṭa poruṇmiyum e-ṟu.
aviyāvaṉa mēṟ collappaṭṭa muṉṉuṟa vuṇartal, kuṟiyuṟa vuṇartaṟkaṇ perumiyiṟ peyarttal, ulakurit toḻittal, arumiyiṉakaṟṟal, piṉ vāveṉṟal, pētimi yūṭṭal, muṉṉuṟu puṇarcci muṟiniṟut turittal, añci yaccuṟuttal, urittuḻik kūṭṭam eṉac collappaṭṭa eṇvaki, māyañ ceppi vanta kiḻavaṉip poṟutta kāraṇam kuṟittalākiya iruvarumuḷvaḻi avaṉ vara vuṇartal, puṇarntapiṉ avaṉvayiṉ vaṇaṅkal, kuṟinayappac cēṟal, kuṟinayappuvaki, nayantami kūṟal, alarāmeṉṟal, puṇarcci vēṇṭiyavaḻik kūṟal, pirivu vēṇṭiyavaḻik kūṟal, vēḷāṇ peruneṟi vēṇṭik kūṟal, allakuṟip paṭṭavaḻik kūṟal, ōmpaṭi kūṟal, iyaṟpaḻittu vaṟpuṟuttal, āṟiṉṉāmi kūṟal, kāppu mikuti kūṟal, kātal mikuti kūṟal, avaṉvayiṟṟōṉṟiya kiḷavi, aiyacceyki tāykketir maṟuttal, kuṟipārttal vilakkal, veṟivilakkal, piṟaṉ varivu maṟuppittal, avaṉ varivuṭampaṭuttal, varivuṭampaṭṭami talivaṟkuk kūṟal, uṭampaṭṭami talivikkuk kūṟi vaṟpuṟuttal eṉa ivi.
tāṅkaruñ ciṟappiṟ ṟōḻi mēṉa eṉpatu  ivi muppattiraṇṭu poruṇmiyum talivikku iṉṟiyamiyāta tōḻi mēlaṉa e-ṟu.	(24)
kaḷavil cevilikku uriyakiḷavi
113.	kaḷavala rāyiṉuṅ kāmamēṟ paṭuppiṉum
	aḷavumikat tōṉṟiṉun talippeytu kāṇiṉuṅ
	kaṭṭiṉuṅ kaḻaṅkiṉum veṟiyeṉa iruvarum
	oṭṭiya tiṟattāṟ ceytik kaṇṇum
	āṭiya ceṉṟuḻi aḻivutali variṉuṅ
	kātal kimmikak kaṉaviṉ araṟṟalun
	tōḻiyi viṉātalun teyvam vāḻttalum
	pōkkuṭaṉ aṟintapiṉ tōḻiyoṭu keḻīik
	kaṟpiṉ ākkattu niṟṟaṟ kaṇṇum
	piriviṉ eccattu makaḷneñcu valippiṉum
	irupāṟ kuṭipporuḷ iyalpiṉ kaṇṇum
	iṉṉa vakiyiṟ patiṉmūṉṟu kiḷaviyōṭu
	aṉṉavi piṟavuñ cevili mēṉa.
eṉṟatu mēṟ ṟalivaṟkun talivikkun tōḻikku muriya kiḷaviyellāṅ kūṟi, iṉic cevilikkuriya kiḷavi yuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
kaḷavalarātal mutalākac collappaṭṭa patiṉmūṉṟu kiḷaviyum attaṉmiya piṟa kiḷaviyum kaḷavukkālattuc ceviliyiṉ mēlaṉa e-ṟu. ivaṟṟuḷ tōḻiyi viṉātaleṉa vēṟoru kiḷaviyāka ōtiṉā rāyiṉum, ataṉ muṉpu nikaḻum kiḷaviyellām avaḷi viṉātaṟkuk kāraṇa mātaliṉ avi yīṇṭup patiṉmūṉṟeṉa veṇṇappaṭṭaṉa veṉka.
(i-ḷ.) kaḷavala rāyiṉum eṉpatu  talivaṉ oḻukalāṟu puṟattārkkup pulaṉāki alar tūṟṟappaṭṭa viṭattut tōḻiyi viṉāvum e-ṟu.
u-m:
''pāvaṭi urala pakuvāy vaḷḷi
ētiṉ mākkaḷ nuvaṟalum nuvalpa
aḻiva tevaṉkolip pēti ūrkkē
perumpūṭ poṟiyaṉ pēemutir kollik
karuṅkaṭ ṭeyvaṅ kuṭavari eḻutiya
nalliyaṟ pāvi aṉṉa eṉ
melliyaṟ kuṟumakaḷ pāṭiṉaḷ kuṟiṉē.''	(kuṟun. 89)
kāma mēṟ paṭuppiṉum eṉpatu  talivimāṭṭuḷatākiya vēṭki aḷaviṟappiṉum tōḻiyi viṉāvum e-ṟu.
''maṇiyil tikaḻtaru nūlpōl maṭanti
aṇiyil tikaḻvatoṉ ṟuṇṭu.''	(kuṟaḷ. 1273)
aḷavu mikat tōṉṟiṉum eṉpatu  petumpip paruvattaḷākiya talivi puṇarcciyāṟ katirttu vīṅkukiṉṟa muliyum putituṟṟa kaviṉuṅ kaṇṭaviṭattun tōḻiyi viṉāvum e-ṟu.
''kaṇniṟinta kārikik kāmpērtōṭ pētikkup
peṇniṟinta nīrmi peritu.''	(kuṟaḷ. 1272)
talippeytu kāṇiṉum eṉpatu  talivaṉoṭu taliviyit talippeytu kāṇiṉum viṉāvum e-ṟu.
peyteṉpataṉip peyaveṉat tirikka.
''...............................................
miṭiyūr piḻiyak kaṇṭaṉeṉ''	(akam. 158)
eṉa varum.
kaṭṭiṉum eṉpatu  kaṭṭu vippittavaḻiyum avarcoṟ kēṭṭut tōḻiyi viṉāvum e-ṟu.
kaḻaṅkiṉum eṉpatu  kaḻaṅku vittuḻiyum avarcoṟ kēṭṭut tōḻiyi viṉāvum e-ṟu.
veṟiyeṉa viruvaru moṭṭiya tiṟattāṟ ceytikkaṇṇum eṉpatu  ceviliyum naṟṟāyum poruntiya pakkattuk kaṇṭu veṟi yāṭuvāmeṉṟavaḻit talivi ceytikkaṇṇun tōḻiyi viṉāvum e-ṟu.
āṭiya ceṉṟuḻi aḻivu talivariṉum eṉpatu  veṟiyāṭiya ceṉṟavaḻi ataṟkaḻivuṟumiṭattu variṉum e-ṟu.
aḵtāvatu: ''kaṭavuṭ kaṟcuṉi'' eṉat toṭaṅkum naṟṟiṇip pāṭṭil,
''niṉṉaṇaṅ kaṉmi aṟintum aṇṇāntu
kārnaṟuṅ kaṭampiṉ kaṇṇi cūṭi
vēlaṉ vēṇṭa veṟimaṉi vantōy
kaṭavu ḷāyiṉum āka
maṭavi maṉṟa vāḻiya murukē.''	(naṟ. 34)
eṉat tōḻi kūṟutal. avvāṟu kūṟiyavaḻiyuṅ kāraṇa meṉṉiyeṉa viṉāvum.
kātal kimmikak kaḷavi ṉaraṟṟalum eṉpatu kātaṉ mikutiyāṉ talivaṉi yuḷḷik kaṉaviṉkaṇ talivi araṟṟutaṟkaṇṇum viṉāvum.
tōḻiyi viṉātalum eṉpatu  ivi nimittamākat tōḻiyi viṉātalum e-ṟu. eṉavē kaḷavalarātal mutaṟ kaṉaviṉaraṟṟa līṟāka ōtiya voṉpatu kiḷaviyum tōḻiyi viṉātaṟ pakuti. avi nikaḻātavaḻi viṉātalilli. ataṉāṉ tōḻiyi viṉātaleṉa oru kiḷaviyāka eṇṇaṟka.
teyvam vāḻttalum eṉpatu  ivvāṟu paṭṭateṉat tōḻiyurittavaḻi yitaṉi naṟṟāykkum tantikkum kūṟalāṟṟātāḷ teyvatti vēṇṭikkōṭal.
pōkkuṭaṉ aṟintapiṉ... niṟṟaṟ kaṇṇum eṉpatu  talivaṉuṭaṉ pōyiṉāḷ eṉṟu aṟintavaḻit tāṉun tōḻiyoṭu keḻumi illattiṉka ṇiṟuttaṟkaṇṇum e-ṟu.
''paṟipaṭap paṇilam ārppa iṟikoḷpu
toṉmū tālattup potiyil tōṉṟiya
nālūrk kōcar naṉmoḻi pōla
vāyā kiṉṟē tōḻi āykaḻaṟ
cēyili veḷvēl viṭaliyoṭu
tokuvaḷi muṉki maṭanti naṭpē.''	(kuṟun. 15)
pirivi ṉeccattu makaṇeñcu valippiṉum eṉpatu  talivaṉ variyātu pirintavaḻi yoḻinta talimakaḷ alarākutalu miṉṟi vēṟupāṭumiṉṟi orumaṉippaṭṭirunta vuḷḷakkarutti yaṟintavaḻiyum e-ṟu. valittal eṉpatu teḷital.
irupāṟ kuṭipporu ḷiyalpiṉkaṇṇum eṉpatu talivaṉ kuṭimi taṉ kuṭimiyō ṭokkumeṉa vārāytaṟkaṇṇum e-ṟu.
kuṭiyeṉṉātu poruḷ eṉṟataṉāṉ poruḷuṅ kuṇamum āyappeṟumeṉṟavāṟu.
'aṉṉavi piṟavum' eṉṟataṉāṉ,
''nāṟṟam peṟṟu nilippuk kāṇṭal
uṇṭiyiṟ kuṟital uṭampunaṉi curuṅkal
kaṇtuyil maṟuttal kōlañcey yāmi''
mutalāyiṉa koḷka. iviyum viṉātaṟkētuvām. ivaṟṟiṟ kellāñ ceyyuḷ vantavaḻik kāṇka.	(25)
naṟṟāykku uriyaṉa
114. tāykkum variyār uṇarvuṭam paṭiṉē.
itu naṟṟāykku uriyatōr ilakkaṇamuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ceviliyuṇarvōṭu uṭampaṭṭa vuḷḷattaḷāyiṉ, naṟṟāykkum mēṟcollappaṭṭavi yellām variyappaṭā veṉṟavāṟu.
uṇarvuṭampaṭutalāvatu  taliviyi yuṟṟu nōkkum nōkkam nikaḻntavaḻiyaṉṟit tāṉum ceviliyip pōla uṟṟu nōkkā ḷeṉṟu koḷka. ipporuḷmēṟ kiḷavi varuvaṉa uḷavēṉuñ ceviliyip pōla voruppaṭṭa vuḷḷattaḷāyiṉ avaḷkaṇṇum ikkiḷavi yellām nikaḻum e-ṟu. uṭampaṭātavāṟu eṉṉi yeṉiṉ, yāriṭattum makkaḷi vaḷarppār ceviliyarākalāṉum tamakkut tam illaṟanilikkuk kaṭavap pakutiyāṉa aṟaṉum poruḷum iṉpamum vēṇṭutalāṉum kūṟṟoṭu vēṟupāṭu tōṉṟātu.	(26)
naṟṟāyum ceviliyum tuṇiyumāṟu
115.	kiḻavōṉ aṟiyā aṟiviṉaḷ ivaḷeṉa
	miyaṟu ciṟappiṉ uyarntōr pāṅkiṉ
	aiyak kiḷaviyiṉ aṟitalum urittē.
itu naṟṟāyum ceviliyum tuṇiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) kiḻavōṉ aṟiyā aṟiviṉaḷ eṉpatu  talimakaṉ aṟiyā aṟiviṉiyuṭiyavaḷ e-ṟu. eṉavē oru pakkam etirkālam nōkkik kūṟiṉār pōlat tōṉṟum; oru pakkam iṟanta kālam tōṉṟum. avaṉ aṟiyāta aṟivurimi pūṇṭu mayaṅkutal, avaḷ ettuṇiyum mayakkamilaḷ eṉavum avaṉporuṭṭu mayaṅki ṉāḷ eṉavumpaṭak kūṟutal. talivaṉ aṟiyāta aṟiviṉi yuṭiyavaḷ eṉak kuṟṟamaṟṟa ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya uyarntōrmāṭṭu uḷatākiya aiyak kiḷaviyāṉ puṇarppaṟitalum urittu, cevilikkum naṟṟāykkum e-ṟu.
iḵtu eṟṟiṉāṉ āyiṟṟu eṉak kuṟṟamaṟṟa tavari viṉāyavaḻi avar ivvāṟu paṭṭateṉa mey kūṟutalun takutiyaṉṟām; poy kūṟutalum takutiyaṉṟām. ātalāl aiyappaṭumāṟu cila kūṟiyavaḻi, ataṉāṉē yuṇarpa e-ṟu. 'kiḻavō ṉaṟiyā vaṟiviṉa ḷeṉṟavāṟu kūṟiyavaḻik kiḻavō ṉetirppaṭa... iṟanta kālattuḷ talivaṉ uḷaṉ eṉṟavāṟām.	(27)
talivikku uriyatōr iyalpu
116.	taṉṉuṟu vēṭki kiḻavaṉmuṟ kiḷattal
	eṇṇuṅ kālik kiḻattik killi
	piṟanīr mākkaḷiṉ aṟiya āyiṭip
	peynnīr pōlum uṇarviṟ ṟeṉpa.
itu, talivikku uriyatōr iyalpuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) (talivi) taṉatu vēṭkiyik kiḻavaṉ muṉpu collutal niṉikkuṅ kālattuk kiḻattikku illi. aṅṅaṉam collāta viṭattum putukkalattiṉkaṭ peyta nīr pōlap puṟam pocintu kāṭṭum uṇarviṉiyu muṭittu avvēṭki e-ṟu.
eṉavē, kuṟippiṉ uṇaraniṟkum e-ṟu. talivaṉmāṭṭuk kūṟṟiṉāṉum nikaḻappaṭumeṉṟu koḷḷappaṭum.	(28)
kaḷaviṟ puṇarccikku uriyatoru vēṟupāṭu
117.	kāmak kūṭṭan taṉimiyiṟ politaliṉ
	tāmē tūtuva rākalum urittē.
eṉṟatu, kaḷaviṟpuṇarccik kuriyatoru vēṟupāṭuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) mēṟ coṉṉavāṟṟāṉ pāṅkaṉun tōḻiyu nimittamā kak kūṭutalēyaṉṟit tāmē tūtuvarākiya kūṭṭaṅkaḷ nikaḻappeṟum, atu ciṟappuṭit tātalāṉ e-ṟu.
eṉavē, pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭam eṉpaṉa niyama milli, yārmāṭṭum eṉṟavāṟām. taṉimiyiṟ politaliṉ eṉṟamiyāṉ itu mikavum naṉṟu.	(29)
talivi kuṟiyiṭam kūṟal
118.	avaṉvaram piṟatta laṟantaṉak kiṉmiyiṉ
	kaḷañcuṭṭuk kiḷavi kiḻaviya tākun
	tāṉcelaṟ kuriyavaḻi yāka lāṉa.
itu, collappaṭṭa kūṭṭattiṟkuk kuṟiyiṭam kūṟuvā ṉuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) talivaṉ iyaṟkippuṇarcci puṇarnta piṉpu, taliva ṉelliyi iṟattal talivikku aṟamāka liṉmiyāṉē kuṟiyiṭam kūṟutal talimakaḷatām, atu tāṉ cēṟaṟkuriya iṭamātalāṉ e-ṟu. 	(30)
eṉavē, ittuṇik kūṟiṉ mikiyaṉṟu eṉṟavāṟām.
tōḻiyiṟkūṭṭac ciṟappuviti
119. tōḻiyiṉ muṭiyum iṭaṉumār uṇṭē.
itu, tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭattiṟ kāyatoru ciṟappuviti uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) mēl, 'kāmakkūṭṭan taṉimiyiṟ politaliṟ ṟāmē tūtuva rākalum urit' teṉak kūṟippōntār; avvāṟaṉṟi mēṟ collappaṭṭa iyaṟkip puṇarcciyāvatu tōḻiyiṉ muṭiyumiṭattu ōriṭattu uṇṭu e-ṟu. ''aṉpoṭu puṇarnta vintiṇi'' (kaḷaviyal. 1) eṉṟataṉāṉ yāṇṭum uḷḷap puṇarcciyāṉ vēṭki mītūrnta vaḻiyē tōḻiyiṉ muṭiyap peṟuvatu eṉṟu koḷka. allākkāṟ peruntiṇippāṟ paṭum.	(31)
pāṅkaṟkūṭṭam nikaḻiṭam
120.	munnā ḷallatu tuṇiyiṉṟu kaḻiyātu
	annā ḷakattum atuvari viṉṟē.
itu, pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam nikaḻumiṭam uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) mēl, tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭattiṉ vikaṟpaṅ kūṟiṉārākaliṉ, īṇṭut tuṇiyeṉṟatu pāṅkaṉ āyiṟṟu. mūṉṟu nāḷallatu tuṇi yiṉṟik kaḷaviṟ puṇarcci cellātu; annāḷakattum tuṇiyi nīkkavum paṭātu e-ṟu.
eṉavē, etirppaṭṭa talivaṉ iyaṟkip puṇarcci puṇarvataṉ muṉ pāṅkaṟku uṇarttavum peṟum e-ṟu.	(32)
utāraṇam mēṟkāṭṭappaṭṭatu.
talivikku uriyatōr iyalpu
121.	paṉṉūṟu vakiyiṉun taṉvayiṉ varūum
	naṉṉaya maruṅkiṉ nāṭṭam vēṇṭaliṉ
	tuṇiccuṭṭuk kiḷavi kiḻaviya tākun
	tuṇiyōr karuma māta lāṉa.
itu, talivikku uriyatō riyalpu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) paṉṉūṟu vakiyiṉum eṉpatu  palavakiyāṉum e-ṟu. nūṟu, pattu, āyiram eṉpaṉa palporuṭ peyar.
taṉvayiṉvarūum ātalāṉa eṉpatu  taṉṉiṭattu varum nalla nayappāṭṭup pakkattiṉi ārāytal talivaṉmāṭṭu vēṇṭu mātalāṉ. tuṇiyic cuṭṭik kūṟaluṟum col talimakaḷa tākum; tāṉ kūṟum karumam tuṇiyōrāṟ ceyyappaṭum karumamā talāṉ e-ṟu.
eṉavē, talimakaṉ kaḷavuk kālattup pāṅkaṟku uṟṟaturitta piṉpu pāṅkaṉic cuṭṭi yātu ceyvāmeṉak kūṟappeṟum eṉṟavā ṟāyiṟṟu.	(33)
cevilikku uriya ciṟappu
122.	āyperuñ ciṟappiṉ arumaṟi kiḷattaliṉ
	tāyeṉap paṭuvōḷ cevili yākum.
itu, cevilikku uriyatoru ciṟappu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) nalla periya ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya aṟitaṟkariya maṟip poruḷ yāvaṟṟiyuṅ kūṟum kaṭappāṭuṭiyaḷātaliṉ tāy eṉappaṭuvōḷ ceviliyākum e-ṟu.
naṟṟāy ittuṇic ciṟappilaḷ e-ṟu. itaṉāṟ payaṉ kaḷavuk kālattiya col cevilittāykkuṅ kittāykkum potuvāyiṉum, tāyeṉṟu vēṇṭappaṭuvāḷ cevili eṉṟaṟivittal.	(34)
tōḻikku uriya ciṟappu
123. tōḻi tāṉē cevili makaḷē.
itu, tōḻikku uriyatoru ciṟappu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kaḷavuk kālattum iṉṟiyamiyāḷākat taliviyāṉ vēṇṭappaṭṭāḷ cevilimakaḷ e-ṟu.
eṉavē, payiṉṟā rellārun tōḻiyarākār. arumaṟi kiḷakkap paṭutalāṉ uṭaṉ muliyuṇṭu vaḷarnta cevili makaḷē tōḻi eṉappaṭuvāḷ e-ṟu. arumaṟi kiḷattal eṉpataṉi yīṇṭu varuvikka.	(35)
tōḻikku uriya iyalpu
124. cūḻtalum ucāttuṇi nilimiyiṟ polimē.
itu, tōḻikku uriyatōr iyalpu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) mēṟcollappaṭṭa tōḻi tāṉ cūḻtaṟkum talivi cūḻccikku ucāttuṇiyākiyum varum nilimiyāṟ polivu peṟum e-ṟu.
eṉavē, cevilimakaḷ eṉṉun tuṇiyāṟ polivupeṟāḷ; eṉṟum tōḻiyāvāḷ cevilimakaḷātalē yaṉṟic cūḻavum ucāt tuṇiyākavum vallaḷ ātalvēṇṭum e-ṟu.	(36)
ceyyuḷ mēṟkāṭṭappaṭṭaṉa.
kaḷaviṉit tōḻi aṟiyum tiṟaṉ
125.	kuṟiyuṟa uṇartal muṉṉuṟa uṇartal
	 iruvarum uḷvaḻi avaṉvara vuṇartaleṉa
	 matiyuṭam paṭutal orumū vakittē.
itu, talivaṉ puṇarcci yuṇmi tōḻi aṟiyun tiṟaṉ pākupaṭu māṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) talivaṉ kuṟiyuṟa vuṇartalum, avaṉ kuṟiyuṟāvaḻit talivi kuṟippuk kaṇṭu uṇartalum, tāṉum taliviyuṅ kūṭiyirun tuḻit talivaṉ vantamikaṇṭu uṇartalum, eṉa mūvakittut tōḻi aṟivuṭampaṭutaṟkaṇṇatu e-ṟu.
matiyumpaṭutal eṉiṉum puṇarcciyuṇartal eṉiṉum okkum. im mūṉṟiṉum oṉṟu kaṇṭuḻi avaravar kuṟippiṉāṟ puṇarcci yuṇarum e-ṟu. 'kuṟiyuṟayuṇartal' muṉvittār, naṉku pulappaṭutaliṉ. 'muṉṉuṟavuṇartal' ataṉpiṉ vittār, talivi vēṟupāṭu kaṇṭu paṇṭiyiṟpōlāḷ eṉṉum nikaḻcciyāṉ muṟṟat tuṇiviṉmiyiṉ. 'iruvaru muḷvaḻi yavaṉ varavuṇartal' ataṉ piṉ vittār, āṇṭup putuvōṉ pōlat talivaṉ varutalāṉum talivi karanta uḷḷattaḷāy niṟkumātalāṉum attuṇip pulappāṭiṉmiyiṉ. akkaruttiṉāṉē mēṟ collappaṭṭa tōḻikūṟṟu mūvakiyākap poruḷ uritta teṉṟu koḷka.	(37)
tōḻikku uriyatoru tiṟaṉ
126.	aṉṉa vakiyāṉ uṇarntapiṉ allatu
	piṉṉili muyaṟci peṟāḷeṉa moḻipa.
ituvum tōḻikku uriyatoru tiṟaṉ uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) mēṟcollappaṭṭa kūṟupāṭṭāṉ iruvarmāṭṭum aṉpuṭimi uṇarntapiṉ allatu vaḻipāṭṭu nilimiyāṟ kūṭṭattiṟku muyalappeṟāḷ tōḻi e-ṟu.
aḵtēl, uḷḷap puṇarcciyāṉiṉṟu meyyuṟātu kūṭṭattiṟku muyalvār uḷar. āyiṉ, aḵteṟṟāṟpeṟum eṉiṉ, āṇṭum iruvar māṭṭuḷatākiya aṉpuṭimiyāṉ maṉanikaḻcci yuḷavāka, annikaḻcci kaṇṭuḻiyum muyalappeṟumeṉṟu koḷka. ataṉāṉēyaṉṟē 'muṉṉuṟa vuṇartal' (125) eṉṉum cūttirattiṉum 'puṇarcci yuṭam paṭutal' eṉṉātu 'matiyuṭampaṭuta loru mūvakittu' eṉap potuppaṭa ōtuvārāyiṟ ṟeṉka. avvaṉpiṉāṉ varu nikaḻcci yuḷḷavaḻiyum ivviṭa mūṉṟiṉum kātaluṇmi aṟiya lākum.	(38)
ituvumatu
127.	muyaṟcik kālat tataṟpaṭa nāṭip
	puṇartta lāṟṟalum avaḷvayi ṉāṉa.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) tōḻi vaḻimoḻintu muyaluṅkālattu avaṉ niṉiviṉkaṭ paṭuntiṟaṉ ārāyntu puṇarttalic ceyyum avaḷiṭattu e-ṟu.
aḵtāvatu, 'iṉṉuḻic cel' eṉavum, 'iṉṉuḻi vā' eṉavum, taliviyi āyattuṇiṉṟum pirittut taṉi niṟuttip paṭṭāṅku kūṟiyum piṟavāṟṟāṉum ārāyntu kūṭṭutal.
ivvintu cūttirattāṉun tōḻikku uriya marapu uṇarttiya vāṟu kāṇka.	(39)
kuṟikku ilakkaṇam
128.	kuṟiyeṉap paṭuva tiraviṉum pakaliṉum
	aṟiyak kiḷanta āṟṟa teṉpa.
eṉṟatu, mēl 'kaḷañcuṭṭuk kiḷavi kiḻaviyatākum' (118) eṉṟār. ataṟku ilakkaṇam uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kuṟi eṉṟu collappaṭuvatu iraviṉāṉum pakaliṉāṉum iruvarum aṟiyac collappaṭṭa iṭattiyuṭittu e-ṟu.
eṉavē, iraviṟkuṟi pakaṟkuṟi eṉa iruvakippaṭum eṉpatu koḷḷappaṭum.	(40)
iravukkuṟikku iṭam
129.	iravuk kuṟiyē illakat tuḷḷum
	maṉiyōr kiḷavi kēṭkumvaḻi yatuvē
	maṉiyakam pukāak kāli yāṉa.
eṉṟatu, iravuk kuṟikku iṭamuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) iravuk kuṟiyām iṭamē illakattuḷ maṉiyakam pukāviṭattukkaṇ maṉiyōr kiḷavi kēṭkum aṇimittām e-ṟu.
eṉavē, maṉikkum eyiṟkum naṭuvaṇatōriṭam eṉṟu koḷḷappaṭum.	(41)
pakaṟkuṟi āmāṟu
130.	pakaṟpuṇar kaḷaṉē puṟaṉeṉa moḻipa
	avaḷaṟi vuṇara varuvaḻi yāṉa.
eṉṟatu, pakaṟkuṟi yāmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) pakaṟkuṟiyākiya puṇarumiṭam eyiṟpuṟaṉ eṉṟu col luvar; āṇṭun talimakaḷ aṟivuṟṟu varum iṭaṉākal vēṇṭum e-ṟu.	(42)
allakuṟippaṭutal
131.	allakuṟip paṭutalum avaḷvayiṉ urittē
	avaṉkuṟi mayaṅkiya amivoṭu variṉē.
ituvumatu.
(i-ḷ.) allakuṟippaṭutalun talimakaṭku urittu; talivaṉ ceyta kuṟi mayaṅkiya poruttattoṭuvariṉ e-ṟu.
utāraṇam mēṟkāṭṭappaṭṭatu.
mayaṅkiya amivu āvatu  avaṉ ceyyum kuṟiyōṭami vuṭiyaṉa.	(43)
ituvumatu
132.	āṅkāṅ koḻukum oḻukkamum uṇṭē
	ōṅkiya ciṟappiṉ oruciṟi yāṉa.
ituvumatu.
(i-ḷ.) avvavviṭat toḻukum oḻukkamun talivimāṭṭu uṇṭu, ōṅkiya ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya oru pakkattu e-ṟu.
oruciṟi yeṉṟatu, maṉattāṉum moḻiyāṉum meyyāṉum kaṟpuṭi makaḷir oḻukum oḻukkattiṉkaṇ maṉattāṉ oḻukum oḻukkamum uṇṭu e-ṟu.	(44)
talivaṟku uriya iyalpu
133.	maṟinta oḻukkat tōriyum nāḷun
	tuṟanta oḻukkaṅ kiḻavaṟ killi.
eṉṟatu, talivaṟku uriya iyalpu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kaḷavoḻukkattu mukurttamum nāḷum tuṟantoḻukum oḻukkam talivaṟku illi e-ṟu.
eṉṟataṉāṉ, āṇṭu aṟattiṉ vaḻuviṉāṉallaṉ, talivi māṭṭut taliyaḷi kuṟitalāṉ e-ṟu.	(45)
ituvumatu
134.	āṟiṉa tarumiyum aḻivum accamum
	ūṟum uḷappaṭa ataṉō raṟṟē.
ituvumatu.
(i-ḷ.) neṟiyiṉatu arumiyum, maṉaṉ aḻivum, añcutalum (45), iṭiyūṟum, talivaṉmāṭṭu nikaḻā e-ṟu.	(46)
tantiyum taṉṉiyum kaḷavu uṇarumāṟu
135. tantiyun taṉṉiyum muṉṉattiṉ uṇarpa.
eṉṟatu, tantiyum, taṉṉiyarum kaḷavu uṇarumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) tantiyarum taṉṉiyarum kuṟippiṉ uṇarpa e-ṟu.
eṉavē, kūṟṟiṉāṉ urikkappeṟār eṉṟavāṟām.	(47)
naṟṟāykku uriyatoru marapu
136. tāy aṟivuṟutal ceviliyō ṭokkum.
eṉṟatu, naṟṟāykku uriyatoru marapuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) naṟṟāy kaḷavoḻukkam aṟivuṟutal ceviliyō ṭokkum e-ṟu. cevili kavaluntuṇik kavalutalallatu, tantiyiyum taṉṉiyaṉmāriyum pōla vekuṭalilaḷ e-ṟu. avar vekuḷvarō eṉiṉ.
''kāmar kaṭumpuṉal'' eṉat toṭaṅkum kalittokip pāṭṭiṉuḷ (39),
''aṟattoṭu niṉṟēṉik kaṇṭu tiṟappaṭa
eṉṉiyark kuytturittāḷ yāy.''	(kalit. 39)
eṉat tāy vekuḷāmi kāṇappaṭṭatu.
''avarum terikaṇi nōkkic cilinōkkik kaṇcēntu
orupaka lellām urutteḻun tāṟi''	(kalit. 39)
eṉṟataṉāṉ vekuṭci peṟṟām.	(48)
ampalum alarum
137.	ampalum alaruṅ kaḷavuveḷip paṭuttaliṉ
	aṅkataṉ mutalvaṉ kiḻava ṉākum.
eṉṟatu, kaḷavu veḷippaṭuppāṉ talivaṉ eṉpatu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ampal eṉpatu mukiḻttal. aḵtu oruvaroruvar mukakkuṟippiṉāṟ ṟōṟṟuvittal. alarāvatu collutal. ataṉāṉē ivi iraṇṭum kaḷaviṉi veḷippaṭuttaliṉ ataṟkuk kāraṇa māvāṉ talivaṉ e-ṟu. eṉṉi? avaṉi yaṟintuḻiyallatu ivi nikaḻāmiyiṉ. talivi vēṟupāṭṭāṉ ākātō eṉiṉ, āṇṭu eṟṟiṉāṉ āyiṟṟu eṉa aiyam nikaḻtalallatu, tuṇivu piṟavātām eṉṟu koḷka.	(49)
variyum pakuti
138.	veḷippaṭa varital paṭāmi varitaleṉṟu
	āyiraṇ ṭeṉpa varital āṟē.
itu, variyum pakuti uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kaḷavu veḷippaṭṭapiṉ varitalum kaḷavu veḷip paṭāmi varitalum, eṉa avviraṇṭeṉṟu colluvar variyum neṟi e-ṟu.
itaṉāṉ colliyatu, iruvāṟṟāṉum aṟaṉ iḻukkāteṉṟa vāṟu. aḵtu iḻukkātavāṟu varukiṉṟa cūttirattāṉ uṇarka.	(50)
talivaṟku uriyatōr iyalpu
139.	veḷippaṭi tāṉē kaṟpiṉō ṭoppiṉum
	ñāṅkark kiḷanta mūṉṟuporu ḷāka
	variyātu pirital kiḻavōṟ killi.
itu, talivaṟku uriyatōr iyalpu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kaḷavu veḷippaṭutal kaṟpiṉōṭoppiṉum, mēṟ collappaṭṭa mūṉṟu poruḷāka variyātu pirital kiḻavōṟku illi e-ṟu.
aviyāvaṉa muṟkūṟiya ōtaṟ pirivum, pakivayiṟ pirivum, tūtiṟ pirivum. eṉavē poruḷvayiṟ piritalum vēntarkkuṟṟuḻip piritalum kāvaṟ piritalum nikaḻappeṟum eṉṟavāṟām.	(51)

mūṉṟāvatu kaḷaviyal muṟṟiṟṟu.

4
kaṟpiyal
ivvōttu eṉṉa peyarttō veṉiṉ, kaṟpiyal eṉṉum peyarttu. kaṟpukku ilakkaṇam uṇarttiṉamiyāṟ peṟṟa peyar. kaṟpeṉpatu yātōveṉiṉ, aḵtāmāṟu iccūttirattiṉ viḷaṅkum.
kaṟpu ilakkaṇam
140.	kaṟpeṉap paṭuvatu karaṇamoṭu puṇarak
	koḷaṟkuri marapiṟ kiḻavaṉ kiḻattiyik
	koṭikkuri marapiṉōr koṭuppakkoḷ vatuvē.
eṉpatu cūttiram.
iccūttiram eṉṉutaliṟṟō veṉiṉ, kaṟpilakkaṇam kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kaṟpeṉṟu collappaṭuvatu, karaṇattoṭu poruntik koḷḷutaṟkuriya marapiṉiyuṭiya kiḻavaṉ koḷḷutaṟkuriya marapiṉiyuṭiya kiḻattiyik koṭuttaṟkuriya marapiṉiyuṭiyār koṭuppak koḷvatu e-ṟu.
'koḷaṟkuriya marapiṉ' eṉpataṉik 'kiḻatti' eṉpataṉoṭuṅ kūṭṭiyurikka.
kaḷaviṉkaṇ ottāriruvar vēṭki mikutiyāṟ kūṭi oḻukiyavaḻik karaṇattiṉ amiyātu illaṟam naṭattalāmō eṉiṉ, aḵtākā teṉṟaṟkuk 'karaṇamoṭu puṇara' eṉṟār. karaṇam eṉpatu  vatuvic caṭaṅku. 'koḷaṟkuri marapiṟ kiḻavōṉ' eṉṟataṉāṉ otta kulattāṉum uyarnta kulattāṉum eṉṟu koḷka. 'koḷaṟkuri marapiṟ kiḻatti' yeṉṟataṉāṉ, otta kulattāḷum iḻinta kulattāḷum eṉṟu koḷka. 'koṭikkuri marapiṉōr' eṉṟataṉāṉ, tantiyum tāyum taṉṉiyarum mātulaṉum ivarillātavaḻic cāṉṟōrum teyvamum eṉṟu koḷka. koṭuppak koḷvatu kaṟpu eṉṟamiyāṉ, atu koṭukkuṅkāl, kaḷavu veḷippaṭṭa vaḻiyum, kaḷavu veḷippaṭāta vaḻiyum, meyyuṟu puṇarcciyiṉṟi uḷ ḷap puṇarcciyāṉ urimipūṇṭa vaḻiyum koḷḷap peṟum eṉak koḷka. kaḷaviyaṟ cūttirattuḷ,
''iṉpamum poruḷu maṟaṉu meṉṟāṅ
kaṉpoṭu puṇarnta.''	(kaḷaviyal 1)
eṉpataṉit tanturittu, aintiṇi maruṅkiṟ kaṟpeṉappaṭuvatu eṉak kūṭṭuka.
aḵtēl, koṭuppak koḷvatu kaṟpāyiṉ piramam mutaliya eṇvakiyum koḷka. 'koṭuppō riṉṟiyum karaṇa muṇṭē  puṇarntuṭaṉ pōkiya kāli yāṉa' (141) eṉṉum itu kaṟpākumō eṉiṉ, ākum. aviyum kaṟpātal okkumēṉum kantaruvampōla otta aṉpuṭiyār ātal orutaliyaṉmiyiṉ kikkiḷi perun tiṇippāṟpaṭum. īṇṭu aintiṇi taḻuviya akattiṇiyiyē kaḷavu kaṟpu eṉap pakuttār eṉṟu koḷka.	(1)
mēlataṟkup puṟaṉaṭi
141.	koṭuppōr iṉṟiyuṅ karaṇa muṇṭē
	puṇarntuṭaṉ pōkiya kāli yāṉa.
itu mēlataṟkoru puṟaṉaṭi.
(i-ḷ.) koṭuppōriṉṟiyum, karaṇanikaḻcci uṇṭu, puṇarn tuṭaṉ pōkiya kālattu e-ṟu.
eṉavē kaṟpiṟkuk karaṇanikaḻcci orutaliyāyiṟṟu. itaṉāṉē koṭuppō rilvaḻiyum karaṇanikaḻcci uṇmiyum oḻukkak kuṟipāṭu iṉmiyum koḷka.
''paṟipaṭap paṇilam ārppa iṟikoḷpu
tolmū tālattup potiyil tōṉṟiya
nālūrk kōcar naṉmoḻi pōla
vāyā kiṉṟē tōḻi yāykaḻaṟ
cēyili veḷvēl viṭaliyoṭu
tokuvaḷi muṉki maṭanti naṭpē.'' 	(kuṟun. 15)
itaṉuḷ viṭaliyoṭu maṭanti naṭpu paṟipaṭap paṇilam ārppa iṟikoṇṭu nālūrk kōcar naṉmoḻipōla vāyāyiṟṟu eṉac cevili naṟṟāykkuk kūṟiṉamiyāṉum, viṭali eṉap pāli nilattiṟkuriya talivaṉ peyar kūṟiṉamiyāṉum koṭup pōriṉṟi yum karaṇam nikaḻntavāṟu kāṇka.	(2)
ituvumatu
142.	mēlōr mūvarkkum puṇartta karaṇaṅ
	kīḻōrk kākiya kālamum uṇṭē.
ituvumatu.
(i-ḷ.) mēṟkulattārākiya antaṇar aracar vaṇikar eṉṉum mūṉṟu varuṇattārkkum puṇartta karaṇam kīḻōrākiya vēḷāṇ māntarkkum ākiya kālamum uṇṭu e-ṟu.
itaṉāṟ colliyatu, muṟkālattuk karaṇam potuppaṭa nikaḻta liṉ ellārkkum ām eṉpatum, piṟkālattu vēḷāṇ māntarkkut tavirntateṉavuṅ kūṟiyavāṟu pōlum. aḵtāmāṟu taruma cāttiram vallārikkoṇṭuṇarka.	(3)
karaṇamākiyavāṟu
143.	poyyum vaḻuvun tōṉṟiya piṉṉar
	aiyar yāttaṉar karaṇam eṉpa.
eṉṟatu, karaṇamākiyavāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) poykūṟalum vaḻūuppaṭa voḻukalum tōṉṟiya piṉṉar muṉivar karaṇattik kaṭṭiṉār eṉṟu colvar e-ṟu.
iraṇṭun tōṉṟuvatu iraṇṭām ūḻiyiṉkaṇṇātaliṉ, mutalūḻiyiṟ karaṇamiṉṟiyē ilvāḻkki naṭantateṉpatūum ivi tōṉṟiya piṉṉark karaṇan tōṉṟiṉa teṉpatūum kūṟiyavāṟāyiṟṟu. poyyāvatu ceytataṉi maṟittal. vaḻuvāvatu ceytataṉkaṇ muṭiya nillātu tappi yoḻukutal. karaṇattoṭu muṭinta kāliyiṉ avi yiraṇṭum nikaḻāvā mātalāṟ karaṇam vēṇṭuvatāyiṟṟu.	(4)
kaṟpil talivaṟku uriya kiḷavi
144.	karaṇattiṉ amintu muṭinta kāli
	neñcutaḷi aviḻnta puṇarccik kaṇṇum
	eñcā makiḻcci iṟantuvaru paruvattum
	añca vanta urimik kaṇṇum
	naṉṉeṟip paṭarun toṉṉalap poruḷiṉum
	peṟṟa tēettup perumiyiṉ niliik
	kuṟṟañ cāṉṟa poruḷeṭut turippiṉum
	nāmak kālat tuṇṭeṉat tōḻi
	ēmuṟu kaṭavuḷ ēttiya maruṅkiṉum
	allal tīra ārvamo ṭaḷiic
	colluṟu poruḷiṉ kaṇṇuñ colleṉa
	ēṉatu cuvippiṉum nīki toṭṭatu
	vāṉōr amiḻtam puriyumāl emakkeṉa
	aṭicilum pūvun toṭuttaṟ kaṇṇum
	antaṇar tiṟattum cāṉṟōr tēettum
	antamil ciṟappiṟ piṟarpiṟar tiṟattiṉum
	oḻukkaṅ kāṭṭiya kuṟippiṉum oḻukkattuk
	kaḷaviṉuḷ nikaḻnta arumiyip pulampi
	alamara luḷḷamo ṭaḷaviya iṭattum
	antarat teḻutiya eḻuttiṉ māṉa
	vanta kuṟṟam vaḻikeṭa oḻukalum
	aḻiyal añcaleṉ ṟāyiru poruḷiṉun
	tāṉavaṭ piḻitta paruvat tāṉum
	nōṉmiyum perumiyum meykoḷa varuḷip
	paṉṉal cāṉṟa vāyiloṭu poruntit
	taṉṉi ṉākiya takutik kaṇṇum
	putalvaṟ payanta puṉiṟutīr poḻutiṉ
	neyyaṇi mayakkam purintōḷ nōkki
	aiyar pāṅkiṉum amararc cuṭṭiyuñ
	ceyperuñ ciṟappoṭu cērtaṟ kaṇṇum
	payaṅkeḻu tuṇiyaṇip pullip pullātu
	uyaṅkuvaṉaḷ kiṭanta kiḻattiyik kuṟuki
	alkal muṉṉiya niṟiyaḻi poḻutiṉ
	melleṉ cīṟaṭi pulliya iraviṉum
	uṟalaruṅ kurimiyiṉ ūṭaṉmikut tōḷip
	piṟapiṟa peṇṭiriṟ peyarttaṟ kaṇṇum
	piriviṉ eccattup pulampiya iruvarip
	parivu nīkkiya pakutik kaṇṇum
	niṉṟunaṉi piriviṉ añciya piyuḷuñ
	ceṉṟuki ikantupeyart tuḷḷiya vaḻiyuṅ
	kāmattiṉ valiyuṅ kiviṭiṉ accamun
	tāṉavaṭ piḻitta niliyiṉ kaṇṇum
	uṭaṉcēṟaṟ ceykiyō ṭaṉṉavi piṟavum
	maṭampaṭa vanta tōḻik kaṇṇum
	vēṟṟunāṭ ṭakalvayiṉ viḻumat tāṉum
	mīṭṭuvara vāynta vakiyiṉ kaṇṇum
	avvaḻip perukiya ciṟappiṉ kaṇṇum
	pērici yūrtip pākar pāṅkiṉuṅ
	kāmak kiḻatti maṉiyōḷ eṉṟivar
	ēmuṟu kiḷavi colliya etiruñ
	ceṉṟa tēet tuḻappunaṉi viḷakki
	iṉṟic ceṉṟa taṉṉili kiḷappiṉum
	aruntoḻil muṭitta cemmaṟ kāli
	viruntoṭu nallavi vēṇṭaṟ kaṇṇum
	māli yēntiya peṇṭirum makkaḷuṅ
	kēḷir oḻukkattup pukaṟcik kaṇṇum
	ēṉiya vāyilō retiroṭu tokiip
	paṇṇami pakutimup patiṉoru mūṉṟum
	eṇṇaruñ ciṟappiṟ kiḻavōṉ mēṉa.
itu, talivaṟku uriya kiḷaviyellān tokuttuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) karaṇattiṉamintu muṭinta piṉpu, neñcutaḷi yaviḻnta puṇarcci mutalāka ēṉiya vāyilōretiroṭu kūṭip paṇṇuta laminta pakutiyiṉiyuṭiya muppatiṉ mūṉṟiṭattiṉum kūṟal eṇṇutaṟ kariya ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya kiḻavōṉ mēlaṉa e-ṟu.
iṭam eṉpatu vakiyiṟ kūṟiyavataṉāṉ urikkappaṭṭatu. kūṟṟeṉpatu varukiṉṟa cūttirattiṉum koṇarnturikkappaṭṭatu.
karaṇatti ṉamintu muṭinta kāli eṉpatu  ācāṉ puṇartta karaṇattiṉāṉ vatuvi muṭintapiṉ e-ṟu.
neñcu taḷiyaviḻta lāvatu  taliviyit talivaṉ kaṇṇuṟṟa ñāṉṟu talivaṉmāṭṭu uḷavākiya perumiyum uraṉum talivi māṭṭu uḷavākiya accamum nāṇum maṭaṉum ētuvāka iyaṟkip puṇarcci iṭiyīṭupaṭṭuḻi, vēṭki taṇiyātu varinteytuṅkāṟum iruvarmāṭṭum kaṭṭuṇṭu niṉṟa neñcam kaṭṭuviṭappaṭutal. iyaṟkip puṇarcci puṇarnta talivaṉ alaraṟi vuṟuttappaṭṭu nīṅki varinteytuṅkāṟum, puṇarcci vēṭkiyāṟ celkiṉṟa neñciṉi iruvarum vēṭki tōṟṟāmal taḷikkap paṭṭataṉit taḷi eṉṟalum oṉṟu. ivi yiraṇṭiṉum mikuti poruḷākak koḷka.
u-m:
''uḻuntu talippeyta koḻuṅkaḷi mitavi
peruñcōṟ ṟamali niṟpa nirikāl
taṇperum pantart tarumaṇal ñemiri
maṉiviḷak kuṟuttu māli toṭarik
kaṉiyiruḷ akaṉṟa kaviṉpeṟu kālik
kōḷkāl nīṅkiya koṭuveṇ tiṅkaḷ
kēṭil viḻuppukaḻ nāḷtali vanteṉa
uccik kuṭattar puttakal maṇṭiyar
potucey kampali mutucem peṇṭir
muṉṉavum piṉṉavum muṟimuṟi tarattarap
putalvarp payanta titaliyav vayiṟṟu
vāliḻi makaḷir nālvar kūṭik
kaṟpiṉiṉ vaḻāa naṟpala utavip
peṟṟōṟ peṭkum piṇiyi ākeṉa
nīroṭu corinta īritaḻ alari
palliruṅ katuppiṉ nelloṭu tayaṅka
vatuvi naṉmaṇaṅ kaḻinta piṉṟik
kalleṉ cummiyar ñerēreṉap pukutantu
pēriṟ kiḻatti yākeṉat tamartara
ōriṟ kūṭiya vuṭaṉpuṇar kaṅkuṟ
koṭumpuṟam vaḷiik kōṭik kaliṅkattu
oṭuṅkiṉaḷ kiṭantaṉaḷ ōrpuṟan taḻīi 
muyaṅkal viruppoṭu mukamputi tiṟappa
añciṉaḷ uyirtta kāli yāḻaniṉ
neñcam paṭarntatu eñcā turiyeṉa
iṉṉaki irukkip piṉyāṉ viṉavaliṉ
ceñcūṭ ṭoṇkuḻi vaṇkātu tuyalvara
akamali uvakiyaḷ ākimukaṉ ikuttu
oyyeṉa iṟiñci yōḷē māviṉ
maṭaṅkoḷ matiiya nōkkiṉ
oṭuṅkī rōti māa yōḷē.''	(akam. 86)
itaṉuḷ 'muyaṅkal viruppoṭu mukamputi tiṟappa añciṉaḷ uyirttakāli' eṉpataṉāṉ iyaṟkippuṇarcci yiṉmiyum, 'akamaliyu vakiyaḷāki mukaṉikuttu oyyeṉa viṟiñci' eṉpataṉāṉ uḷḷappuṇarcci yuṇmiyum aṟika. piṟavum aṉṉa.
eñcā makiḻcci yiṟantuvaru paruvattum eṉpatu  oḻiyāta makiḻcci mikku varuṅ kālattut talivaṉkaṭ kūṟṟu nikaḻum e-ṟu.
u-m:
''kuṉikā yerukkiṉ kuvimukiḻ viṇṭaloṭu
paṉivār āvirip paṉmalar cērttit
tāruṅ kaṇṇiyun tatiit taṉṉiṭṭu
ūrum maṭavōṉ ularvaṉ kolleṉa
nīrttuṟip peṇṭir neñcaḻin tiraṅkiṉum
uṇarā ḷūrtōṟu
aṇimaṭaṟ kalimā maṉṟat tēṟittaṉ
aṇinalam pāṭiṉum aṟiyā ḷeṉṟiyāṉ
perumali neṭuṅkō ṭēṟip peṟukeṉṟu
urumiṭit tīyiṉ uṭampucuṭar vitta
eṉṉuṟu viḻumam nōkkip poṉṉoṭu
tirumaṇi imikkuṅ kōṭuyar naṉantali
iravuṭip peṇṭi riṭumpi nōkkit
teḷivumaṉaṅ koṇṭa tītaṟu kāṭci
veḷiyaṉ vēḷmāṉ viḷaṅkukari pōla
malikaṭal uṭutta maṇaṅkeḻu naṉantalip
palapā rāṭṭavum paṭuva mātō
kaṭintu kavittaṉṉa kālvīṅku karuṅkaṭ
puṭitiraḷ vaṉamuli pulampal añcik
kāmar nuḻinuṇ nucuppiṉ
tāmari mukattiyit tanta pālē.''	(kuṇanāṟpatu.)
eṉa varum.
añcavanta urimikkaṇṇum eṉṟatu  talivaṉ tāṉum piṟarum añcumpaṭiyākat talivimāṭṭu uḷatākiya kaṟpākiya urimikkaṇ ṇum e-ṟu.
utāraṇam vantavaḻik kāṇka.
naṉṉeṟip paṭarum toṉṉalap poruḷiṉum eṉṟatu  naṉṉeṟikkaṭ cellāniṉṟa toṉṉalap poruṇmikkaṇṇum e-ṟu.
naṉṉeṟiyāvatu aṟamporuḷiṉpam vaḻuvāta neṟi. talimakaṉ ciṟapput toṉṟutoṭṭu varutaliṟ kuṭinalattit toṉṉalameṉṟār. itaṉāṟ colliyatu aṟam poruḷ iṉpaṅkaḷi vaḻāmal taṉkulat tiṟkēṟṟa maṉivāḻkkiyit talimakaḷ naṭattutaṟkaṇṇum talivaṉkaṇ kūṟṟu nikaḻum e-ṟu.
u-m:
''taṭamarup perumi maṭinaṭik kuḻavi
tūṇtoṟum yātta kāṇtaku nallil
koṭuṅkuḻi peyta ceḻuñcey pēti
ciṟutāḻ ceṟitta melviral cēppa
vāḻi īrntaṭi vallitiṉ vakiip
pukiyuṇ ṭamartta kaṇṇaḷ takipeṟap
piṟinutaṟ poṟitta ciṟunuṇ palviyar
antukil taliyil tuṭiyiṉaḷ nappulantu
aṭṭi lōḷē ammā arivi
emakkē varukatil viruntē civappāḷaṉṟu
ciṟiyamuḷ eyiṟu tōṉṟa
muṟuval koṇṭa mukaṅkāṇ kammē.''	(naṟ. 120)
itaṉuḷ ūṭaṟkuṟippiṉa ḷākiya talivi maṉivāḻkkit tarumamākiya viruntu puṟantarutal viruppiṉaḷātaliṉ naṉṉeṟip paṭartal āyiṟṟu.
peṟṟa tēettup perumiyiṉ niliik kuṟṟañ cāṉṟa poruḷ eṭutturippiṉum eṉṟatu  varintu peṟṟavaḻit taliviyip perumiyiṉkaṇṇē niṟuttik kaḷavukkālattuk kuṟṟañcāṉṟa poruḷi yeṭuttuk kūṟiyavaḻiyum e-ṟu.
u-m:
''atirici yaruviya peruvarit toṭutta
paltēṉ iṟāal alkunark kutavu
nunti naṉṉāṭṭu ventiṟal murukeṉa
niṉṉōyk kiyaṟṟiya veṟiniṉ tōḻi
eṉvayi ṉōkkaliṟ pōlum paṉṉāḷ
varuntiya varuttan tīraniṉ
tiruntiḻip paṇittōḷ puṇarntuvan tatuvē.''
itaṉuḷ 'nunti naṉṉāṭṭu' eṉṟataṉāṉ talivi perumiyum, 'niṉṉōyk kiyaṟṟiya veṟi niṉtōḻi yeṉvayi ṉōkkaliṟ pōlum' eṉṟataṉāl kuṟṟañcāṉṟa poruḷ eṉpatum aṟintukoḷka.
nāmak kālat tuṇṭeṉat tōḻi ēmuṟu kaṭavuḷ ēttiya maruṅkiṉum eṉpatu  accakkālattu namakkut tuṇiyāyiṟṟeṉat tōḻi ēmuṟu kaṭavuḷi ēttutaṟkaṇṇum talivaṉkaṭ kūṟṟu nikaḻum e-ṟu.
utāraṇam vantavaḻik kāṇka.
allal tīra ārvamoṭu aḷiic colluṟu poruḷiṉ kaṇṇum eṉpatu  talivi taṉ tuṉpan tīra ārvattoṭu poruntac collappaṭṭa poruṇmikkaṇṇum talivaṉ kūṟṟu nikaḻum e-ṟu. eṉṟatu kaḷavuk kālattu varuntiya varuttan tīrat taṉatu kātal mikuti tōṉṟac collutaṟ poruḷiṉkaṇṇum e-ṟu.
u-m:
''yāyum ñāyum yārā kiyarō
entiyum nuntiyum emmuṟik kēḷir
yāṉum nīyum evvaḻi aṟituñ
cempulap peyaṉīr pōla
aṉpuṭi neñcan tāṅkalan taṉavē.''	(kuṟun. 40)
eṉa varum.
colleṉa ēṉatu cuvippiṉum nī ki toṭṭatu vāṉōr amiḻtam puriyumāl emakkeṉa aṭicilum pūvun toṭuttaṟkaṇ ṇum eṉpatu  yātāṉum oṉṟi nukariṉum nī kiyāṉ toṭṭatu vāṉōr amiḻtam puriyum, itaṟkuk kāraṇam colluvāyāka eṉṟu aṭicil toṭuttaṟkaṇṇum pūttoṭuttaṟkaṇṇum kūṟṟu nikaḻum e-ṟu. vantatu koṇṭu vārātatu muṭittal eṉpataṉāṉ, cāntu mutaliyaṉavum koḷka.
utāraṇam vantavaḻik kāṇka.
''vēmpiṉ piṅkāyeṉ tōḻi tariṉē
tēmpūṅ kaṭṭi''	(kuṟun. 196)
eṉat talivaṉ kūṟiṉami tōḻi kūṟalāṉum aṟika.
antaṇar tiṟattum cāṉṟōr tēettum antamil ciṟappiṟ piṟar piṟar tiṟattiṉum oḻukkam kāṭṭiya kuṟippiṉum eṉpatu  pārppār kaṇṇum cāṉṟōrkaṇṇum mikka ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya piṟarākiya avaravariṭattum oḻukum oḻukkattik kuṟippiṉāṉ kāṭṭiya iṭattiṉum e-ṟu.
utāraṇam vantavaḻik kaṇṭukoḷka.
oḻukkattuk kaḷaviṉuḷ nikaḻnta arumiyip pulampi alamara luḷḷamō ṭaḷaviyaviṭattum eṉpatu  oḻukkattiṉuṅ kaḷavuk kālattu nikaḻnta arumiyit taṉittuc cuḻaṉṟa uḷḷattōṭē ucāviya viṭattum e-ṟu.
utāraṇam vantavaḻik kāṇka.
antarat teḻutiya veḻuttiṉ māṉa vanta kuṟṟam vaḻi keṭa oḻukalum eṉpatu  kaḷavukkālat toḻukiya oḻukkak kuṟipāṭṭāṉ nikaḻnta kuṟṟatti ākāyatteḻuttup pōla vaḻikeṭa oḻuku taṟkaṇṇum e-ṟu.
utāraṇam vantavaḻik kāṇka.
aḻiyal añcaleṉ ṟāyiru poruḷiṉun tāṉavaṭ piḻitta paruvattāṉum eṉpatu  aḻiyal, añcal eṉa iyaṟkip puṇarc cikkaṭ kūṟiya avviruporuḷip piḻitta kālattiṉum talivaṉ kaṇ kūṟṟu nikaḻum e-ṟu.
aḵtāvatu, puṟappeṇṭirmāṭṭup pirital.
''nakukam vārāy pāṇa pakuvāy
aripey kiṇkiṇi ārppat teruvil
tērnaṭi payiṟṟun tēmoḻip putalvaṉ
pūnāṟu cevvāy cititta cāntamoṭu
kāma neñcan turappa yāntaṉ
muyaṅkal viruppoṭu kuṟukiṉēm ākap
piṟivaṉap puṟṟa mācaṟu tirunutal
nāṟiruṅ katuppiṉeṅ kātali vēṟuṇarntu
verūumāṉ piṇiyiṉ orīi
yāri yōveṉṟu ikantuniṉ ṟatuvē.''	(naṟ. 250)
eṉa varum.
nōṉmiyum perumiyum meykoḷa varuḷip paṉṉal cāṉṟa vāyiloṭu poruntit taṉṉiṉākiya takutikkaṇṇum eṉpatu  poṟiyum perumiyum meyyeṉak koḷḷumāṟu aruḷi, ārāytal aminta vāyiloṭu poruntit, talivaṉ taṉṉāṉ ākiya takutikkaṇṇum kūṟṟu nikaḻum e-ṟu.
aruḷip poruntik kūṟum eṉak kūṭṭuka. eṉavē talimakaṉ eṉpatūun talimakaḷ eṉpatūum eñciniṉṟaṉa. kūṟṟu eṉṟatu atikārattāṉ vantatu. aḵtāvatu, poṟuttal vēṇṭum eṉavum ciṟumi ceytal kuṟṟam eṉavum kūṟutalum, talimakaḷtaṉṉāṉ vantataṉi eṉṉāṉ vantatu eṉavum ivvāṟu kūṟutal. paṉṉal cāṉṟa vāyilāvatu, ''nī eṉ ceytaṉi? ivaḷ vekuṭaṟkuk kāraṇam eṉṉi?'' eṉa ārāytaliṟ poruntiya tōḻi eṉka. poruntalāvatu vēṟupaṭātu uṭampaṭutal. avi varumāṟu:
''yāriṉuṅ kātalam eṉṟēṉā ūṭiṉāḷ
yāriṉum yāriṉum eṉṟu.''	(kuṟaḷ. 1314)
''tummuc ceṟuppa aḻutāḷ numaruḷḷal
emmi maṟittirō eṉṟu.''	(kuṟaḷ. 1318)
''immip piṟappil piriyalam eṉṟēṉāk
kaṇṇiṟi nīrkoṇ ṭaṉaḷ.''	(kuṟaḷ. 1315)
''taṉṉi yuṇarttiṉuṅ kāyum piṟarkkunīr
innīrar ākutir eṉṟu.''	(kuṟaḷ. 1319)
''kōṭṭuppūc cūṭiṉuṅ kāyum oruttiyik
kāṭṭiya cūṭiṉīr eṉṟu.''	(kuṟaḷ. 1313)
eṉa varum.
piṟavum aṉṉa.
putalvaṟ payanta puṉiṟutīr poḻutiṉ neyyaṇi mayakkam purintōḷ nōkki aiyar pāṅkiṉum amararc cuṭṭiyuñ ceyperuñ ciṟappoṭu cērtaṟkaṇṇum eṉpatu  putalvaṉip payanta īṉṟa ṇimi nīṅkiṉa poḻutiṉkaṇ neyyaṇi mayakkam purintavaḷik kuṟittu muṉivarmāṭṭum amararik kuṟittum ceyyāniṟkum periya ciṟappoṭu cērtaṟkaṇṇum kūṟṟu nikaḻum e-ṟu.
neyyaṇi mayakkamāvatu vālāmi nīṅki neyyaṇital. nōkkic cērtal eṉak kūṭṭuka.
''vārāy pāṇa nakukam nēriḻi
kaṭumpuṭik kaṭuñcūl naṅkuṭik kutavi
neyyō ṭimikkum aiyavit tiraḷkāḻ
viḷaṅkunar viḷaṅkak kiṭantōṭ kuṟukip
putalvaṉi yīṉṟeṉap peyarpeyarttu avvarit
titali alkul mutupeṇ ṭākit
tuñcuti yōmel añcil ōtiyeṉap
paṉmāṇ akaṭṭiṟ kuvaḷi oṟṟi
uḷḷiṉeṉ uṟiyum eṟkaṇṭu mella
mukināḷ muṟuval tōṟṟit
takimalar uṇkaṇ putittuvan tatuvē.''	(naṟ. 370) 
eṉa varum.
payaṅkeḻu tuṇiyaṇip pullip pullātu uyaṅkuvaṉaḷ kiṭanta kiḻattiyik kuṟukiya alkal muṉṉiya niṟiyaḻipoḻutiṉ melleṉ cīṟaṭi pulliya iraviṉum eṉpatu  talivaṉ parattiyiṟ pirintuḻi ūṭaṟ karuttiṉaḷāyp payaṅkeḻu tuṇi aṇiyip pullip pullātu varuntik kiṭanta taliviyik kiṭṭit taṅkutalik kuṟitta niṟiyaḻipoḻutil taliviyatu melleṉṟa cīṟaṭiyip pulliya irattaṟkaṇṇum talivaṉ kūṟṟu nikaḻum e-ṟu.
u-m:
''ūṭaliṉ uṇṭāṅkōr tuṉpam puṇarvatu
nīṭuva taṉṟukol eṉṟu.''	(kuṟaḷ. 1307)
''ūṭuka maṉṉō oḷiyiḻi yāmirappa
nīṭuka maṉṉō irā.''	(kuṟaḷ. 1329)
''ūṭalil tōṉṟuñ ciṟutuṉi nallaḷi
vāṭiṉum pāṭu peṟum.''	(kuṟaḷ. 1322)
eṉa varum.
uṟalaruṅ kurimiyi ṉūṭaṉ mikuttōḷip piṟapiṟa peṇṭiriṟ peyarttaṟkaṇṇum eṉpatu  ūṭal mikuttōḷi uṟutaṟkarumiyāṟ piṟapiṟa peṇṭir ētuvāka ūṭal uṇarttaṟkaṇṇum e-ṟu.
''puṉavaḷar pūṅkoṭi yaṉṉāy'' eṉṉum marutak kaliyuḷ,
''orutti, pulaviyāṟ pullā tiruntāḷ alavuṟṟu
vaṇṭiṉam ārppa iṭiviṭṭuk kātalaṉ
taṇtār akalam pukum.''	(kali. 92)
eṉap piṟapiṟa peṇṭirik kāṭṭit talivaṉ ūṭaluṇarttiyavāṟu aṟintu koḷka.
piriviṉeccattup pulampiya viruvarip parivu nīkkiya pakutik kaṇṇum eṉpatu  pirivu nimittamāka varuntiya maṉiyāḷiyum kāmak kiḻattiyiyum av varuttattu niṉṟu nīkkiya pakutikkaṇṇum kūṟṟu nikaḻum e-ṟu.; aḵtāvatu piriyēṉ eṉṟal.
''poṉṉum maṇiyum pōlum yāḻaniṉ
naṉṉar mēṉiyum nāṟiruṅ katuppum
pōtum paṇiyum pōlum yāḻaniṉ
mātar uṇkaṇum vaṉappiṉ tōḷum
ivikāṇ tōṟum akamalintu yāṉum
aṟanili peṟṟōr aṉiyēṉ ataṉtalip
polantoṭip putalvaṉum poytal kaṟṟaṉaṉ
viṉiyum vēṟupulattu ilaṉē niṉiyiṉ
yātaṉiṟ pirivām maṭanti
kātal tāṉuṅ kaṭaliṉum peritē.''	(naṟ. 166)
ikkūṟṟu iruvarmāṭṭum okkum.
niṉṟunaṉi piriviṉ añciya piyuḷum eṉpatu  niliniṟka mikappiriyum piriviṉkaṇ añciya nōyiṉkaṇṇum kūṟṟu nikaḻum e-ṟu.
''āḷvaḻak kaṟṟa curattiṭik katirteṟa
nīḷeri paranta neṭuntāḷ yāttup
pōyvaḷi muḻaṅkum pulleṉ uyarciṉi 
muṭinaci irukkip peṭimukam nōkki
ūṉpatit taṉṉa veruvaru ceñcevi
eruvic cēval karipuciṟi tīya
vēṉiṉ nīṭiya vēyuyar naṉantali
nīyuḻan teytuñ ceyviṉip poruṭpiṇi
pallitaḻ maḻikkaṇ māa yōḷvayiṟ
piriyiṟ puṇarva tāyiṟ piriyātu
ēntumuli muṟṟam vīṅkap pallūḻ
cēyiḻi teḷirppak kavii nāḷum
maṉimutal viṉiyoṭum uvappa
niṉimāṇ neñcam nīṅkutal maṟantē.''	(akam. 51)
eṉa varum.
ceṉṟu kiyikantu peyarttuḷḷiyavaḻiyum eṉpatu  mēṟkūṟiya vāṟṟiṉik kiyikantu muṉṉorukāṟ ceṉṟu mīṭṭum anneṟi yiṉip pōka niṉintavaḻiyum kūṟṟu nikaḻum e-ṟu.
''iruṅkaḻi mutali mēentōl aṉṉa
karuṅkāl ōmik kāṇpiṉ peruñciṉik 
kaṭiyuṭi naṉantali īṉṟiḷip paṭṭa
koṭuvāyp pēṭik kalkiri tarīiyar
māṉṟuvēṭ ṭeḻunta ceñcevi eruvi
vāṉtōy cimiya viṟalvarik kavāaṉ
tuḷaṅkunaṭi mariyā valampaṭat tolicci
oṇceṅ kuruti yuvaṟiyuṇ ṭaruntupu
pulavuppuli tuṟanta kalavukkaḻi kaṭumuṭi
koḷḷi māntariṉ āṉātu kavarum
pullili marāatta akaṉcēṇ attaṅ
kalantaral uḷḷamoṭu kaḻiyak kāṭṭip
piṉniṉṟu turakkum neñcam niṉvāy
vāypōṟ poymmoḻi evvameṉ kaḷimā
kaviritaḻ aṉṉa kāṇpiṉ cevvāy
antīṅ kiḷavi āyiḻi maṭanti
koṭuṅkuḻik kamartta nōkkam
neṭuñcēṇ āriṭi vilaṅku ñāṉṟē.''	(akam. 3)
eṉa varum.
kāmattiṉ valiyum eṉpatu  poruḷiṉuṅ kāmam valiyuṭittu eṉa uṭkoṇṭavaḻiyum kūṟṟu nikaḻum e-ṟu.
u-m:
''viritirip peruṅkaṭal vaḷiiya ulakamum
aritupeṟu ciṟappiṟ puttēḷ nāṭum
iraṇṭun tūkkiṟ cīrcā lāvē
pūppōl uṇkaṇ poṉpōl mēṉi
māṇvari alkuṟ kuṟumakaḷ
tōḷmāṟu paṭūum vikalō ṭemakkē.''	(kuṟun. 101)
eṉavarum.
kiviṭiṉ accamum eṉpatu taliviyik kiviṭṭavaḻi avaḷatu uyirpporuṭṭu añcutaṟkaṇṇum kūṟṟu nikaḻum e-ṟu.
u-m:
''aḷinili poṟāatu amariya mukattaḷ
viḷinili kēḷāḷ tamiyaḷ meṉmela
nalamiku cēvaṭi nilamvaṭuk koḷāak
kuṟuka vantutaṉ kūreyiṟu tōṉṟa
vaṟitakat teḻunta vāyal muṟuvalaḷ
kaṇṇiya tuṇarā aḷavi oṇṇutal
viṉitalip paṭutal cellā niṉivuṭaṉ
muḷinta ōmi mutiyalaṅ kāṭṭup
paḷiṅkat taṉṉa palkāy nelli
mōṭṭirum pāṟi īṭṭuvaṭ ṭēyppa
utirvaṉa paṭūuṅ katirteṟu kavāaṉ
māytta pōla maḻukunuṉi tōṟṟip
pātti aṉṉa kuṭumik kūrṅkal 
viralnuti citikkum nirinili yatara
paraṉmuram pākiya payamil kāṉam
iṟappa eṇṇuti rāyiṉ aṟattāṟu
aṉṟeṉa moḻinta toṉṟupaṭu kiḷavi
aṉṉa vāka veṉṉunaḷ pōla
muṉṉam kāṭṭi mukattiṉ uriyā
ōvac ceytiyiṉ oṉṟuniṉin toṟṟip
pāvi māytta paṉinīr nōkkamoṭu
ākat toṭukkiya putalvaṉ puṉtalit
tūnīr payanta tuṇiyami piṇiyal
mōyiṉaḷ uyirtta kāli māmalar
maṇiyuru iḻanta aṇiyaḻi tōṟṟam
kaṇṭē kaṭintaṉañ celavē oṇtoṭi
uḻiyam ākavum iṉivōḷ
piḻiyalaḷ mātō piritunām eṉiṉē.''	(akam. 5)
eṉa varum.
tāṉavaṭpiḻitta niliyiṉkaṇṇum eṉpatu  talivaṉ taliviyi 'niṉṉiṟ piriyēṉ' eṉṟa colliṟ piḻitta niliyiṉ kaṇṇum kūṟṟu nikaḻum e-ṟu.
piḻittalāvatu pirital:
''vayaṅkumaṇi poruta vakiyami vaṉappiṟ
pacuṅkāḻ alkul māa yōḷoṭu
viṉivaṉap peytiya puṉipūñ cēkki
viṇporu neṭunakart taṅki iṉṟē
iṉituṭaṉ kaḻintaṉṟu maṉṉē nāḷip
poruntāk kaṇṇēm pulampuvan tuṟutarac
cēkkuvaṅ kollō neñcē cātteṟintu
atarkūṭ ṭuṇṇum aṇaṅkuṭip pakaḻik
koṭuvil āṭavar paṭupaki verīi
ūreḻun tulaṟiya pīreḻu mutupāḻ 
muruṅki mēynta peruṅki yāṉi
verinōṅku ciṟupuṟam uriña olki
iṭṭiki neṭuñcuvar viṭṭam vīḻnteṉa
maṇippuṟāt tuṟanta marañcōr māṭattu
eḻutaṇi kaṭavuḷ pōkaliṟ pulleṉṟu
oḻukupali maṟanta meḻukāp puṉtiṇip
pāṉāy tuṉṉiya paṟikkaṭ ciṟṟil
kuyilkāḻ citiya maṇṭi ayilvāyk
kūrmukac citali vēynta
pōrmaṭi nalliṟip potiyi lāṉē.''	(akam. 167)
eṉa varum.
uṭaṉ cēṟaṟ ceykiyoṭu eṉpatu  uṭaṉpōka vēṇṭu meṉac colliyavaḻiyum e-ṟu. oṭu eṇṇiṉkaṇ vantatu.
''cerumiku ciṉavēntaṉ'' eṉṉum pālik kaliyuḷ,
''elvaḷi emmoṭu nīvariṉ yāḻaniṉ
melliyal mēvanta cīṟaṭit tāmari
allicēr āyitaḻ arakkuttōyn tavipōlak
kalluṟiṉ avvaṭi kaṟukkuna allavō.''	(kalit. 13)
eṉa varum.
aṉṉavi piṟavum maṭampaṭa vanta tōḻikkaṇṇum eṉpatu  mēṟcollappaṭṭaviyiṟṟiṉum maṭamippaṭa vanta tōḻi māṭṭum kūṟṟu nikaḻum e-ṟu.
aviyāvaṉa:
''illeṉa irantōrkkoṉ ṟīyāmi iḻiveṉak
kalliṟantu ceyalcūḻnta poruḷporu ḷākumō''	(kalit.2)
eṉa varum. innikaraṉa kūṟiyavaḻit talivaṉ kūṟṟu nikaḻum. ivvaḻik kūṟuṅ kūṟṟuk kāmamākat tōṉṟātu poruḷākat tōṉṟum, kāmattiṟku māṟākak kūṟal vēṇṭutaliṉ.
''iṉpam viḻiyāṉ viṉiviḻivāṉ taṉkēḷir
tuṉpan tuṭittūṉṟun tūṇ.''	(kuṟaḷ. 615)
eṉa varum.
vēṟṟunāṭ ṭakalvayiṉ viḻumattāṉum eṉpatu  vēṟṟunāṭṭu akalumvaḻi varum nōyiṉkaṇṇum e-ṟu.
aḵtāvatu, pirivu oruppaṭṭa piṉpu pōvēmō tavirvēmō eṉac collum maṉanikaḻcci:
u-m:
''uṇṇā miyiṉ uyaṅkiya maruṅkiṉ
āṭāp paṭivat tāṉṟōr pōla
variceṟi ciṟuneṟi niripuṭaṉ cellum
kāṉa yāṉi kaviṉaḻi kuṉṟam
iṟantu poruḷ tarutalum āṟṟāy ciṟanta
ciliṅ kūntal nallakam porunti
oḻiyiṉ vaṟumi yañcuti aḻitaka
uṭimati vāḻiya neñcē nilaveṉa
neykaṉi neṭuvēl eḵkili imikkum
maḻimaruḷ paḵṟōl māvaṇ cōḻar
kaḻimāy kāvirik kaṭalmaṇṭu peruntuṟi
iṟavoṭu vantu kōtiyoṭu peyarum
peruṅkaṭal ōtam pōla
oṉṟiṟ koḷḷāy ceṉṟutaru poruṭkē.''	(akam. 123)
eṉa varum.
mīṭṭuvara vāynta vakiyiṉ kaṇṇum eṉpatu pirinta talivaṉ mīṭṭu varavu vāynta vakiyiṉkaṇṇum e-ṟu.
'varavu' eṉpatu kaṭikuṟintatu.
u-m:
''tāḻiruḷ tumiya miṉṉit taṇṇeṉa
vūḻuṟi iṉiya citaṟi ūḻiṟ
kaṭippiṭu muraciṉ muḻaṅki iṭittiṭittup
peyyiṉi vāḻiyō peruvāṉ yāmē
ceyviṉi muṭitta cemmal uḷḷamoṭu
ivaḷiṉ mēviṉa mākik kuvaḷik
kuṟuntāḷ nāḷmalar nāṟum
naṟumeṉ kūntal mellaṇi yēmē.''	(kuṟun. 270)
itu vantu pukunta talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
avvaḻip perukiya ciṟappiṉ kaṇṇum eṉpatu  talivaṉ pirintu vantuḻip perukiya ciṟappiṉum kūṟṟu nikaḻum e-ṟu.
''kōṭal etirmukip pacuvī mulli
nāṟitaḻk kuvaḷiyō ṭiṭippaṭa virii
aitutoṭi māṇṭa kōti pōla
naṟiya nallōḷ mēṉi
muṟiyiṉum vāyatu muyaṅkaṟkum iṉitē.''	(kuṟun. 62)
eṉa varum.
pērici yūrtip pākar pāṅkiṉum eṉpatu  tāṉuṟṟa viṉpat tiṉip pākaṟkuk kūṟutaṟkaṇṇum e-ṟu.
u-m:
''maṟattaṟ karitāl pāka paṉṉāḷ
vaṟattoṭu poruntiya ulakutoḻiṟ koḷīiya
paḻamaḻi poḻinta putunīr avala
nānavil palkiḷi kaṟaṅka nāvuṭi
maṇiyoli kēḷāḷ vāṇutal ataṉāl
ēkumiṉ eṉṟa iḷiyar vallē
ilpuk kaṟiyuna rāka melleṉa
maṇṇāk kūntal mācaṟak kaḻīic
cilpōtu koṇṭu palkural aḻuttiya
annili pukutaliṉ meyvarut tuṟāa
aviḻpū muṭiyiṉaḷ kaviiya
maṭamā arivi makiḻntayar niliyē.''	(naṟ. 42)
eṉa varum.
kāmak kiḻatti maṉiyō ḷeṉṟiva rēmuṟu kiḷavi colliya vetirum eṉpatu  kāmakkiḻattiyum maṉiyāḷum eṉṟu collu miruvarum pātukāvalākak kūṟiya kūṟṟiṉetirum talivaṉ kūṟṟu nikaḻum e-ṟu.
